### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/frontend/Component.tsx

```tsx
import type { InteractionProps } from "@platform/interaction-sdk";

/** Shared page/conversation view; the host owns authentication and persistence. */
export default function TextTool({
  state,
  result,
  busy,
  actions,
  updateState,
  invokeAction,
}: InteractionProps) {
  const text = String(state.text ?? "");
  const canRun = actions.some(
    (action) => action.id === "analyze" && action.available,
  );
  const output = result?.data;
  return (
    <section aria-label="文本整理工具">
      <label className="field">
        待整理文本
        <textarea
          aria-label="待整理文本"
          rows={7}
          maxLength={20000}
          value={text}
          disabled={busy}
          onChange={(event) =>
            updateState({ ...state, text: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <p className="subtle">
        最多 20000 个字符。统一换行、整理空白，并保留段落。
      </p>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="button primary"
        disabled={busy || !canRun || !text.trim()}
        onClick={() => void invokeAction("analyze")}
      >
        {busy ? "正在处理…" : "整理与统计"}
      </button>
      {!canRun && <p role="status">当前没有工具使用权限，或模块暂不可用。</p>}
      {output && (
        <div aria-label="文本整理结果" aria-live="polite">
          <p>
            {Number(output.character_count)} 个字符 ·{" "}
            {Number(output.line_count)} 行 · {Number(output.segment_count)}{" "}
            个空白分隔片段
          </p>
          <pre style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>
            {String(output.normalized_text ?? "")}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```json
  "input_schema": {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {},
    "additionalProperties": false
  },
  "state_schema": {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {
      "text": {
        "type": "string",
        "maxLength": 20000
      }
    },
    "required": [
      "text"
    ],
    "additionalProperties": false
  },
  "initial_state": {
    "text": ""
  },
  "actions": [
    {
      "id": "analyze",
      "title": "整理与统计",
      "capability_id": "__MODULE_ID__.analyze",
      "capability_version": "1.0.0",
      "completes": true,
      "bindings": {
        "text": {
          "source": "state",
          "key": "text"
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/tests/self_test.py

```python
"""Offline business/SDK checks; no account, database, server or dependency needed.

Run from any working directory: python <generated-package>/tests/self_test.py
The decision fake checks the SDK boundary, not the platform's policy engine.
The framework integration suite separately verifies real grants and revocation.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from runpy import run_path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import json
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SDK = run_path(str(ROOT / "backend" / "_module_kit.py"))
BUSINESS = run_path(str(ROOT / "backend" / "business.py"))
MANIFEST = json.loads((ROOT / "backend" / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
PERMISSION = MANIFEST["id"] + ".use"


class DecisionFake:
    def __init__(self, allowed=True, organization_id="org-a"):
        self.allowed, self.organization_id, self.calls = allowed, organization_id, []

    def check(self, actor, permission, facts):
        self.calls.append((actor.copy(), permission, facts.copy()))
        return {"allowed": self.allowed if facts == {"organization_id": self.organization_id} else False}


def context(access=None, **actor_fields):
    return SimpleNamespace(actor={"id": "synthetic-user", "organization_id": "org-a", "roles": ["member"], **actor_fields},
                           access=DecisionFake() if access is None else access)


def invoke(ctx, payload=None, handler=None):
    return SDK["run_tool"](ctx, permission=PERMISSION, payload={"text": "example"} if payload is None else payload,
                           handler=BUSINESS["analyze"] if handler is None else handler)


class ToolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def assert_error(self, code, thunk):
        with self.assertRaises(SDK["KitError"]) as caught:
            thunk()
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, code)
        return caught.exception

    def test_normalizes_line_endings_and_reports_exact_counts(self):
        result = invoke(context(), {"text": " 甲\t乙 \r\n\r\n hello   world \r final "})
        self.assertEqual(result["data"], {"normalized_text": "甲 乙\n\nhello world\nfinal", "character_count": 22,
                                          "line_count": 4, "segment_count": 5})
        self.assertIn("22", result["summary"])

    def test_multilingual_count_is_codepoints_not_bytes(self):
        data = invoke(context(), {"text": "中文 🙂"})["data"]
        self.assertEqual(data["character_count"], 4)
        self.assertEqual(data["segment_count"], 2)

    def test_business_is_pure_and_input_is_unchanged(self):
        payload = {"text": " a  b "}
        result = BUSINESS["analyze"](payload)
        self.assertEqual(payload, {"text": " a  b "})
        self.assertEqual(result, BUSINESS["analyze"](payload))

    def test_maximum_input_and_output_fit_declared_schema(self):
        data = invoke(context(), {"text": "字" * 20000})["data"]
        self.assertEqual(data["character_count"], 20000)
        schema = MANIFEST["capabilities"][0]["output_schema"]
        self.assertEqual(set(data), set(schema["required"]))
        for name, value in data.items():
            field = schema["properties"][name]
            self.assertTrue(type(value) is int if field["type"] == "integer" else isinstance(value, str))
            self.assertLessEqual(value if type(value) is int else len(value), field.get("maximum", field.get("maxLength")))

    def test_invalid_inputs_do_not_pass_business_boundary(self):
        for payload in ({}, {"text": ""}, {"text": " \n "}, {"text": None}, {"text": 42}, {"text": "x" * 20001}, {"text": "x", "organization_id": "org-b"}):
            with self.subTest(payload_type=str(type(payload.get("text")))):
                self.assert_error("tool_input_invalid", lambda: invoke(context(), payload))

    def test_scope_facts_come_only_from_authenticated_actor(self):
        ctx = context()
        invoke(ctx)
        self.assertEqual(ctx.access.calls[0][1:], (PERMISSION, {"organization_id": "org-a"}))

    def test_other_organization_is_denied_before_business_runs(self):
        calls = []
        self.assert_error("tool_forbidden", lambda: invoke(context(DecisionFake(organization_id="org-b")), handler=lambda value: calls.append(value)))
        self.assertEqual(calls, [])

    def test_no_global_admin_or_legacy_role_fallback(self):
        self.assert_error("tool_forbidden", lambda: invoke(context(DecisionFake(False), roles=["system_admin", "admin", "member"])))

    def test_missing_identity_or_organization_fails_closed(self):
        for fields in ({"id": ""}, {"enabled": False}, {"organization_id": None}, {"organization_id": " "}, {"organization_id": "*"}):
            ctx = context(**fields)
            with self.subTest(fields=fields):
                with self.assertRaises(SDK["KitError"]):
                    invoke(ctx)
                self.assertEqual(ctx.access.calls, [])

    def test_missing_or_failed_access_service_fails_closed(self):
        self.assert_error("tool_access_unavailable", lambda: invoke(SimpleNamespace(actor=context().actor, access=None)))

        class Unavailable:
            def check(self, *args):
                raise RuntimeError("private service detail")

        error = self.assert_error("tool_access_unavailable", lambda: invoke(context(Unavailable())))
        self.assertNotIn("private", error.message)

    def test_malformed_decision_cannot_be_truthy_allow(self):
        for value in (1, "true", None, [], {}):
            self.assert_error("tool_forbidden", lambda: invoke(context(DecisionFake(value))))

    def test_json_input_and_size_guards(self):
        for payload in ([], {"text": float("nan")}, {"text": "\ud800"}):
            self.assert_error("tool_input_invalid", lambda: invoke(context(), payload))
        self.assert_error("tool_input_too_large", lambda: invoke(context(), {"text": "a" * 100001}))

    def test_result_contract_and_json_guards(self):
        for result in (None, {}, {"summary": "", "data": {}}, {"summary": "ok", "data": []},
                       {"summary": "ok", "data": {}, "token": "not permitted"},
                       {"summary": "ok", "data": {"number": float("inf")}}):
            self.assert_error("tool_result_invalid", lambda: invoke(context(), handler=lambda payload: result))
        self.assert_error("tool_result_too_large", lambda: invoke(context(), handler=lambda payload: {"summary": "ok", "data": {"text": "x" * 256001}}))

    def test_grant_contract_matches_installed_capability(self):
        definition = json.loads((ROOT / "backend" / "access-definition.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(definition["module_id"], MANIFEST["id"])
        self.assertEqual(definition["command_mappings"], [{"capability_id": MANIFEST["id"] + ".analyze", "permission": PERMISSION}])
        self.assertEqual(definition["role_templates"][0]["required_scope"], ["organization_id"])
        self.assertEqual(definition["experiences"], [])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/README.md

````markdown
# __TITLE__

这个参考包演示一条可持久化的项目业务：创建草稿 → 作者编辑、提交 → 另一位审核人审核通过。同一组 React 步骤同时用于平台页面和对话。安装后由平台提供登录、授权、个人草稿、继续办理和聊天分支；服务端只持有正式记录。

## 开发者主要改哪里

| 文件 | 修改内容 |
| --- | --- |
| `backend/business.mjs` | 项目仓储、对象字段、正式状态和业务动作 |
| `backend/manifest.json` | 每项能力的输入、输出和说明 |
| `backend/access-definition.json` | 权限、职责模板、范围和办理入口 |
| `frontend/flow.json` | 共享步骤、动作、页面组合及正式状态机 |
| `frontend/steps.tsx` | 页面与对话复用的业务组件 |
| `sdk/node/` | 可移植的服务协议和事务封装，常规业务开发无需复制修改 |

模块 ID 是 `__MODULE_ID__`，前端安装包名是 `__PACK_NAME__`。正式项目应使用自己的唯一标识，不与其他模块命名空间包含或重叠。

## 职责和范围

| 职责 | 允许动作 | 范围与条件 | 页面和对话 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 提交者 `__MODULE_ID__.author` | 读取、创建、编辑、提交 | 每份 grant 同时包含 project_id 和 organization_id；只能编辑、提交本人草稿 | 选择、填写、审核结果 |
| 审核人 `__MODULE_ID__.reviewer` | 读取、审核 | 同样的完整项目/机构范围；只审核别人提交的记录 | 选择、只读内容、审核 |

本参考明确允许同一授权项目和机构内协作读取记录内容。目录先逐对象中央授权，再分页；不会返回范围外记录数量。提交者拥有其他项目的审核职责时，仍按本次对象事实核验，不能把不同授权的范围拼接。`allowed_actions` 只是状态与作者关系形成的候选，宿主再过滤能力权限；实际执行始终重新中央授权、检查正式状态和 expected_revision。

`frontend/flow.json` 中的 `state_machine` 声明草稿、待审核、已通过及动作之间的对应关系。`backend/business.mjs` 读取这同一声明执行正式状态转移，固定页面和对话也读取同一状态名称；提交后不再允许编辑。框架调试器绘制的是正式状态，用户切换 `pick/edit/review` 导航步骤不会改变业务状态。状态机声明不执行任意表达式，`guard_description` 仅说明条件，实际权限、作者关系、修订和意见校验仍由业务后端执行。增加新动作时同时修改能力、共享步骤、状态机及后端规则并测试，不能只在图上画一条线。

项目与机构关系从受控 `MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE` 加载；浏览器只选择 project_id，不能指定可信 organization_id。已有系统应把此查询改为原项目仓储。修改项目配置后旧对象机构不一致会拒绝读取，不能靠重命名配置把旧数据移交其他机构。

## 独立验证与启动

要求 Node.js 24；无需 npm install。先确认使用正确版本：

```powershell
node --version
node --test tests/workflow.test.mjs
```

测试使用临时数据库、随机端口和合成中央决策 HTTP 服务，结束会清理自己的临时目录。它实际验证 HTTP、SQLite、幂等、重启、并发、权限失败和分页，但不代表已经在真实平台注册或通过生产浏览器验证。

部署时在受保护位置准备项目 JSON，可参考 `backend/projects.example.json` 的结构；合成项目编号不能冒充真实项目。由管理员设置服务进程环境：

```powershell
$env:SERVICE_DATA_DIR = 'D:\private-module-data\__PACK_NAME__'
$env:MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE = 'D:\private-module-config\__PACK_NAME__-projects.json'
$env:PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:8030/api/v1/access/remote-decisions'
# PLATFORM_SERVICE_TOKEN 通过私有部署配置注入，至少 24 字符；不要写进源码或共享脚本。
$env:PORT = '8040'
node backend/server.mjs
```

示例地址和路径须改为实际部署环境。服务只监听回环地址；跨机器部署通过受控网关、TLS 与网络访问策略另行配置。启动只输出就绪端口，不输出凭据。停止使用 Ctrl+C；数据保存在 `records.sqlite`，再次使用同一目录启动会恢复对象和回执。参考包最多 1000 条记录、100 个已配置项目；大规模服务应实现仓储范围查询，不直接增加扫描量。

所有 `/platform/v1/health`、`manifest`、`invoke` 和 `workflow/*` 接口要求私有服务 Bearer。业务还要求平台本次调用的短时 `X-Platform-Access` 票据；单有服务 Bearer 或伪造 actor 不构成业务授权。中央连接失败、过期票据和撤权都拒绝，禁止退回固定角色放行。

## 安装到平台

1. 服务进程启动后，由平台管理员录入受控地址与私有凭据，发现 manifest，保持禁用。
2. 把 `frontend/` 的文件复制到平台 `flows/packs/__PACK_NAME__/`，在 `flows/installed.json` 的 `packs` 追加 `__PACK_NAME__`，保留其他条目。使用宿主 React 和 `@platform/workflow-sdk`，不在此目录另装 React。
3. 按平台规程复核提示词、重建快照、执行一致性检查，类型检查和生产构建，再重启受控平台载入 flow。仅复制文件不会热加载流程；权限附件注册前应确认该流程已被宿主载入。不要用开发服务器成功替代生产验收。
4. 管理员审核并注册 `backend/access-definition.json`。安装定义不自动授予任何用户业务权限。
5. 核验 flow 和能力绑定后执行平台七项接入检查，包含服务身份、接口、模块主动确认及已安装宿主。全部通过后在验收有效期内发布；失败时按报告修复并重新验收。具有 `identity.manage` 的管理者给合成测试用户分别授予提交者、审核人，scope 中同时填入项目和机构的准确值，无通配符。
6. 从“我的业务”或 `/flow __MODULE_ID__.workflow` 分别在页面与对话办理。验证 A 创建、保存和提交，B 用自己的流程审核，A 刷新看到结果；再验证撤权、重新登录、同一实例继续、分支不重放正式写入。

服务被禁用或中央授权变化时由现有平台拒绝旧实例；服务不自建个人会话。恢复同一个私人草稿须使用原 instance_id，跨人交接只共享正式对象，不复制私有对话。

## 边界与渐进适配

已实现：有限项目记录、原子 SQLite 状态/修订/幂等回执、中央范围决策、对象读写、授权后目录分页、共享前端组件。SDK 的事务与回执不覆盖外部写操作；跨服务器事务需要另行设计。

未实现：文件、长任务、明细分页、模型调用、通知、游客、SSO、长期委派和通用跨角色事件调度。未实现的 workflow 方法返回 501。流程导航、聊天分支与“我的业务”由现有主框架提供，不能在此包中伪造其状态。

已有独立服务可先保留原 API 和数据库：将既有对象映射为 projection，将动作包装为 invoke，将项目/机构/作者事实从原仓储读取，再接中央授权。保留原事务，把请求编号和正式写入原子保存。只把业务组件迁入 `frontend/`，不重写登录、聊天、原计算逻辑或全部页面。

交付前记录真实平台版本、提示词指纹和验收证据；独立测试通过与真实宿主联调通过应分开记录。
# 注册标准与握手

backend/registration.json 声明本服务公开的对象、业务步骤、权限及共享流程。SDK 在启动时检查实际函数表；收到平台握手后主动比较完整内容和所需协议，再确认。平台发现与离线 check 均不能代替接入验收，七项通过后才能发布；用户授权另行分配。修改能力、权限或流程时同步 registration.json 和独立测试。
````

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/backend/business.mjs

```javascript
/** Business changes belong here. SDK owns transport/transactions; the platform owns identity and grants. */
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { Store } from "../sdk/node/store.mjs";
import { compile, requireThat, fail } from "../sdk/node/runtime.mjs";

export const MODULE_ID = "__MODULE_ID__";
export const OBJECT_TYPE = `${MODULE_ID}.record`;
// The same installed declaration drives visualization and authoritative state
// changes. It is static trusted module code, never a URL or a user expression.
export const stateMachine = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(new URL("../frontend/flow.json", import.meta.url), "utf8"),
).flows[0].state_machine;
function transitionTarget(state, action) {
  const choices = stateMachine.transitions.filter(
    (item) => item.from === state && item.action_id === action,
  );
  requireThat(
    choices.length === 1,
    "state_conflict",
    "当前状态不允许此操作。",
    409,
  );
  return choices[0].to;
}
const identity = {
  type: "string",
  pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,99}$",
};
const projectCheck = compile({
  type: "array",
  minItems: 1,
  maxItems: 100,
  items: {
    type: "object",
    required: ["project_id", "organization_id", "title"],
    properties: {
      project_id: identity,
      organization_id: identity,
      title: { type: "string", minLength: 1, maxLength: 160 },
    },
    additionalProperties: false,
  },
});
const listCheck = compile({
  type: "object",
  additionalProperties: false,
  properties: {
    object_type: { type: "string", const: OBJECT_TYPE },
    cursor: { type: "string", minLength: 1, maxLength: 2048 },
    limit: { type: "integer", minimum: 1, maximum: 50 },
  },
});
const readCheck = compile({
  type: "object",
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: ["object_ref"],
  properties: {
    object_ref: {
      type: "object",
      additionalProperties: false,
      required: ["object_type", "object_id"],
      properties: {
        object_type: { type: "string", const: OBJECT_TYPE },
        object_id: {
          type: "string",
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$",
        },
      },
    },
  },
});
const ordinaryDenials = new Set([
  "grant_scope_mismatch",
  "actor_relation_mismatch",
  "separation_of_duties",
  "fact_condition_failed",
]);
export function createBusiness({ filename, projects, decide }) {
  requireThat(
    projectCheck(projects) &&
      new Set(projects.map((item) => item.project_id)).size === projects.length,
    "project_config",
    "项目仓库配置无效或包含重复编号。",
    500,
  );
  // This trusted deployment configuration is the project repository for the reference package.
  // An existing service replaces this lookup with its own repository, never input.organization_id.
  const repository = new Map(
    projects.map((item) => [item.project_id, Object.freeze({ ...item })]),
  );
  const store = new Store(filename);
  store.db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS records(
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, project_id TEXT NOT NULL, organization_id TEXT NOT NULL, author_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL, body TEXT NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
    reviewer_id TEXT, review_note TEXT, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL);`);
  function facts(record) {
    return {
      project_id: record.project_id,
      organization_id: record.organization_id,
      author_id: record.author_id,
    };
  }
  function load(id) {
    const record = store.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM records WHERE id=?").get(id);
    requireThat(
      record &&
        repository.get(record.project_id)?.organization_id ===
          record.organization_id,
      "object_scope",
      "记录不存在或项目配置已变化。",
      404,
    );
    return record;
  }
  async function decision(
    body,
    ticket,
    operation,
    permission,
    trustedFacts,
    optional = false,
  ) {
    const result = await decide({
      ticket,
      requestId: body.request_id,
      operation,
      permission: `${MODULE_ID}.${permission}`,
      facts: trustedFacts,
    });
    requireThat(
      result &&
        typeof result.allowed === "boolean" &&
        Number.isSafeInteger(result.policy_revision),
      "access_denied",
      "中央授权不可用。",
      403,
    );
    if (result.allowed) return result;
    if (
      optional &&
      ordinaryDenials.has(result.reason) &&
      result.policy_revision > 0
    )
      return result;
    throw fail("access_denied", "当前授权不允许此操作，请重新核验授权。", 403);
  }
  function projection(record, actor, summary = false) {
    const own = record.author_id === actor.id;
    const actions = stateMachine.transitions
      .filter(
        (item) =>
          item.from === record.state &&
          (item.action_id === "approve" ? !own : own),
      )
      .map((item) => item.action_id);
    return {
      object_ref: { object_type: OBJECT_TYPE, object_id: record.id },
      revision: record.revision,
      state: record.state,
      data: summary
        ? {
            title: record.title,
            project_id: record.project_id,
            organization_id: record.organization_id,
          }
        : {
            title: record.title,
            body: record.body,
            project_id: record.project_id,
            organization_id: record.organization_id,
            author_id: record.author_id,
            reviewer_id: record.reviewer_id,
            review_note: record.review_note,
          },
      allowed_actions: actions,
      jobs: [],
      updated_at: record.updated_at,
    };
  }
  async function list(body, ticket, operation, payload) {
    requireThat(listCheck(payload), "input_contract", "目录参数无效。");
    let policyRevision;
    const accepted = [];
    // Even an empty database checks a live ticket against configured trusted project facts.
    for (const project of repository.values()) {
      const result = await decision(
        body,
        ticket,
        operation,
        "read",
        {
          project_id: project.project_id,
          organization_id: project.organization_id,
        },
        true,
      );
      requireThat(
        policyRevision === undefined ||
          policyRevision === result.policy_revision,
        "access_denied",
        "读取期间权限定义已变化。",
        403,
      );
      policyRevision = result.policy_revision;
    }
    // Small-reference policy: at most 1000 records. Replace this with repository scope queries at scale.
    // Crucially, authorization happens before pagination and no unauthorized totals are returned.
    for (const record of store.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM records ORDER BY id")
      .all()) {
      if (
        repository.get(record.project_id)?.organization_id !==
        record.organization_id
      )
        continue;
      const result = await decision(
        body,
        ticket,
        operation,
        "read",
        facts(record),
        true,
      );
      requireThat(
        policyRevision === result.policy_revision,
        "access_denied",
        "读取期间权限定义已变化。",
        403,
      );
      if (result.allowed) accepted.push(record);
    }
    const binding = {
      module: MODULE_ID,
      actor: body.context.actor,
      policyRevision,
      snapshot: store.fingerprint(
        accepted.map((record) => [record.id, record.revision]),
      ),
    };
    const offset = store.offset(payload.cursor, binding),
      limit = payload.limit || 20;
    requireThat(
      offset <= accepted.length,
      "cursor_invalid",
      "分页游标超出目录范围。",
    );
    return {
      objects: accepted
        .slice(offset, offset + limit)
        .map((record) => projection(record, body.context.actor, true)),
      next_cursor:
        offset + limit < accepted.length
          ? store.cursor(binding, offset + limit)
          : null,
    };
  }
  async function invoke({ body, ticket }) {
    return store.transaction(async () => {
      const action = body.capability_id.slice(MODULE_ID.length + 1);
      requireThat(
        body.capability_id === `${MODULE_ID}.${action}` &&
          ["create", "edit", "submit", "approve", "read", "list"].includes(
            action,
          ),
        "capability_unknown",
        "未声明的能力。",
      );
      if (action === "list")
        return {
          summary: "已读取授权范围内的项目记录。",
          data: await list(body, ticket, "invoke", body.input),
        };
      if (action === "read") {
        const record = load(body.input.object_id);
        await decision(body, ticket, "invoke", "read", facts(record));
        return {
          summary: "已读取当前记录。",
          data: { projection: projection(record, body.context.actor) },
        };
      }
      const cached = store.receipt(body);
      const record = cached
        ? load(cached.objectId)
        : action === "create"
          ? null
          : load(body.input.object_id);
      const project =
        action === "create"
          ? repository.get(body.input.command.project_id)
          : null;
      requireThat(
        action !== "create" || project,
        "project_unknown",
        "所选项目不在服务端项目仓库。",
      );
      const trustedFacts = record
        ? facts(record)
        : { ...project, author_id: body.context.actor.id };
      delete trustedFacts.title;
      await decision(body, ticket, "invoke", action, trustedFacts);
      if (cached) return cached.response; // Reauthorize every retry, including after restart; never replay a write.
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString();
      let id = record?.id;
      if (action === "create") {
        requireThat(
          store.db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM records").get().n < 1000,
          "capacity_limit",
          "参考包最多保存 1000 条记录，请扩展仓储后继续。",
          413,
        );
        requireThat(
          body.input.command.title.trim().length > 0,
          "title_required",
          "请输入标题。",
        );
        id = randomUUID();
        store.db
          .prepare("INSERT INTO records VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)")
          .run(
            id,
            project.project_id,
            project.organization_id,
            body.context.actor.id,
            body.input.command.title,
            body.input.command.body,
            stateMachine.initial,
            1,
            null,
            null,
            stamp,
          );
      } else {
        requireThat(
          record.revision === body.input.expected_revision,
          "revision_conflict",
          "记录已更新，请重新读取。",
          409,
        );
        const own = record.author_id === body.context.actor.id;
        requireThat(
          action === "approve" ? !own : own,
          "separation_of_duties",
          "当前办理人与记录作者的关系不符合要求。",
          403,
        );
        const nextState = transitionTarget(record.state, action);
        if (action === "edit")
          requireThat(
            body.input.command.title.trim().length > 0,
            "title_required",
            "请输入标题。",
          );
        if (action === "approve")
          requireThat(
            body.input.command.note.trim().length > 0,
            "note_required",
            "请输入审核意见。",
          );
        const changed = store.db
          .prepare(
            "UPDATE records SET title=?,body=?,state=?,revision=revision+1,reviewer_id=?,review_note=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND revision=? AND state=?",
          )
          .run(
            action === "edit" ? body.input.command.title : record.title,
            action === "edit" ? body.input.command.body : record.body,
            nextState,
            action === "approve" ? body.context.actor.id : null,
            action === "approve" ? body.input.command.note : null,
            stamp,
            id,
            body.input.expected_revision,
            record.state,
          );
        requireThat(
          changed.changes === 1,
          "revision_conflict",
          "另一请求已修改记录。",
          409,
        );
      }
      // A final fresh central check precedes the atomic commit. Denial rolls back both object and receipt.
      await decision(body, ticket, "invoke", action, trustedFacts);
      const response = {
        summary: {
          create: "已创建草稿。",
          edit: "已保存记录。",
          submit: "已提交，等待其他审核人办理。",
          approve: "已审核通过。",
        }[action],
        data: { projection: projection(load(id), body.context.actor) },
      };
      store.saveReceipt(body, id, response);
      return response;
    });
  }
  async function workflow({ body, operation, ticket }) {
    return store.transaction(async () => {
      if (operation === "objects.list")
        return list(body, ticket, operation, body.input);
      requireThat(
        operation === "objects.read",
        "workflow_unsupported",
        "此参考包未实现明细分页、文件或长任务适配。",
        501,
      );
      requireThat(
        readCheck(body.input),
        "input_contract",
        "对象引用不符合契约。",
      );
      const record = load(body.input.object_ref.object_id);
      await decision(body, ticket, operation, "read", facts(record));
      return { projection: projection(record, body.context.actor) };
    });
  }
  // The transport dispatches through these exact maps and validates complete
  // manifest coverage before registration; the handshake never calls them.
  const handlers = Object.fromEntries(
    ["create", "edit", "submit", "approve", "read", "list"].map((action) => [
      `${MODULE_ID}.${action}`,
      invoke,
    ]),
  );
  const workflowHandlers = {
    "objects.list": workflow,
    "objects.read": workflow,
  };
  return {
    invoke,
    workflow,
    handlers,
    workflowHandlers,
    close: () => store.close(),
  };
}
```

```json
    "authentication": "call_ticket"
  }
}
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/backend/server.mjs

```javascript
import { readFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { createModuleServer, requireThat } from "../sdk/node/runtime.mjs";
import { createAccessClient } from "../sdk/node/access-client.mjs";
import { createObservationClient } from "../sdk/node/observation.mjs";
import { createBusiness } from "./business.mjs";

const root = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
export const manifest = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(path.join(root, "manifest.json"), "utf8"),
);
export const registration = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(path.join(root, "registration.json"), "utf8"),
);
export const moduleGraph = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(path.join(root, "module-graph.json"), "utf8"),
);
export function createService({
  token,
  callbackUrl,
  filename,
  projects,
  fetchImpl,
  observationUrl,
}) {
  const business = createBusiness({
    filename,
    projects,
    decide: createAccessClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl }),
  });
  try {
    const observer = createObservationClient({
      callbackUrl:
        observationUrl ||
        new URL("/api/v1/runtime-observations/events", callbackUrl).href,
      graph: moduleGraph,
      fetchImpl,
    });
    // Observe only the projection returned after the business transaction. The
    // module names its own formal machine; the transport does not infer one.
    const handlers = Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(business.handlers).map(([id, handler]) => [
        id,
        async (context) => {
          const result = await handler(context);
          if (result.data.projection)
            context.observe?.state(
              "__MODULE_ID__.record-lifecycle",
              result.data.projection,
            );
          return result;
        },
      ]),
    );
    const server = createModuleServer({
      manifest,
      registration,
      token,
      handlers,
      workflowHandlers: business.workflowHandlers,
      graph: moduleGraph,
      observationClient: observer,
    });
    return {
      server,
      close: async () => {
        if (server.listening)
          await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
        await business.close();
        await observer?.flush();
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    void business.close();
    throw error;
  }
}
if (
  process.argv[1] &&
  import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.argv[1])).href
) {
  requireThat(
    process.env.MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE && process.env.SERVICE_DATA_DIR,
    "service_config",
    "请配置 MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE 和 SERVICE_DATA_DIR。",
    500,
  );
  const directory = path.resolve(process.env.SERVICE_DATA_DIR);
  mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const projects = JSON.parse(
    readFileSync(path.resolve(process.env.MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE), "utf8"),
  );
  const service = createService({
    token: process.env.PLATFORM_SERVICE_TOKEN,
    callbackUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL,
    observationUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_OBSERVATION_URL,
    filename: path.join(directory, "records.sqlite"),
    projects,
  });
  const port = Number(process.env.PORT || 0);
  requireThat(
    Number.isInteger(port) && port >= 0 && port <= 65535,
    "service_config",
    "PORT 无效。",
    500,
  );
  service.server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1", () =>
    console.log(
      JSON.stringify({ ready: true, port: service.server.address().port }),
    ),
  );
  for (const signal of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"])
    process.on(signal, () => void service.close().then(() => process.exit(0)));
}
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/frontend/steps.tsx

```tsx
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import type {
  WorkflowStepComponents,
  WorkflowStepProps,
} from "@platform/workflow-sdk";
import definition from "./flow.json";

const objectType = definition.flows[0].provider.object_type;
const stateLabels: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries(
  definition.flows[0].state_machine.states.map((state) => [
    state.id,
    state.title,
  ]),
);
function useAction() {
  const [error, setError] = useState(""),
    [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const running = useRef(false);
  async function run(work: () => Promise<unknown>) {
    if (running.current) return;
    running.current = true;
    setPending(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await work();
    } catch (reason) {
      setError(
        reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : "操作未完成，请重试。",
      );
    } finally {
      running.current = false;
      setPending(false);
    }
  }
  return { error, pending, run };
}
function Picker(props: WorkflowStepProps) {
  const action = useAction(),
    locked = props.busy || action.pending;
  const available = props.actions.some(
    (item) => item.id === "create" && item.available,
  );
  const project_id = String(props.draft.project_id ?? ""),
    title = String(props.draft.title ?? ""),
    body = String(props.draft.body ?? "");
  const records = props.objects.filter(
    (item) => item.object_ref.object_type === objectType,
  );
  const more = props.objectPages.some(
    (item) => item.object_type === objectType && item.next_cursor,
  );
  return (
    <section aria-label="选择或创建记录">
      <h3>当前授权项目的记录</h3>
      <p>
        同项目、同机构授权范围内的参与者可读；编辑、提交和审核另外核验职责。
      </p>
      <ul>
        {records.map((record) => (
          <li key={record.object_ref.object_id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={locked}
              onClick={() =>
                void action.run(() =>
                  props.openObject(record.object_ref.object_id),
                )
              }
            >
              {String(record.data.title)} ·{" "}
              {stateLabels[record.state] ?? record.state}
            </button>{" "}
            <small>{String(record.data.project_id)}</small>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {!records.length && <p>当前没有可读记录。</p>}
      {more && (
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={locked}
          onClick={() =>
            void action.run(() => props.loadMoreObjects(objectType))
          }
        >
          加载更多记录
        </button>
      )}
      <h3>创建草稿</h3>
      <p>项目编号请使用管理员提供的服务端已登记编号。</p>
      <label>
        项目编号
        <input
          aria-label="项目编号"
          value={project_id}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({
              ...props.draft,
              project_id: event.target.value,
            })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        标题
        <input
          aria-label="新记录标题"
          value={title}
          maxLength={200}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, title: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        内容
        <textarea
          aria-label="新记录内容"
          value={body}
          maxLength={4000}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, body: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !available || !title.trim() || !project_id.trim()}
        onClick={() =>
          void action.run(() =>
            props.execute("create", { project_id, title, body }),
          )
        }
      >
        创建记录
      </button>
      {!available && <p>当前没有创建记录权限。</p>}
      {action.error && <p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
function Editor(props: WorkflowStepProps) {
  const action = useAction();
  if (!props.projection)
    return (
      <section>
        <h3>填写与提交</h3>
        <p>请先选择或创建一条记录。</p>
      </section>
    );
  const source = props.projection.data,
    locked = props.busy || action.pending;
  const editable = props.actions.some(
    (item) => item.id === "edit" && item.available,
  );
  const submittable = props.actions.some(
    (item) => item.id === "submit" && item.available,
  );
  const title = String(props.draft.title ?? source.title ?? ""),
    body = String(props.draft.body ?? source.body ?? "");
  const changed =
    title !== String(source.title ?? "") || body !== String(source.body ?? "");
  return (
    <section aria-label="填写与提交">
      <h3>填写与提交</h3>
      <p>
        当前修订 {props.projection.revision} ·{" "}
        {stateLabels[props.projection.state] ?? props.projection.state}
      </p>
      <label>
        标题
        <input
          aria-label="记录标题"
          value={title}
          maxLength={200}
          disabled={locked || !editable}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, title: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        内容
        <textarea
          aria-label="记录内容"
          value={body}
          maxLength={4000}
          disabled={locked || !editable}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, body: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !editable || !title.trim()}
        onClick={() =>
          void action.run(() => props.execute("edit", { title, body }))
        }
      >
        保存记录
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !submittable || changed}
        onClick={() => void action.run(() => props.execute("submit", {}))}
      >
        提交审核
      </button>
      {changed && editable && <p>提交前请先保存正式记录。</p>}
      {!editable && <p>当前记录只读。</p>}
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked}
        onClick={() => props.navigate("review")}
      >
        查看审核与结果
      </button>
      {action.error && <p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
function Review(props: WorkflowStepProps) {
  const action = useAction();
  if (!props.projection)
    return (
      <section>
        <h3>审核与结果</h3>
        <p>请先选择一条记录。</p>
      </section>
    );
  const locked = props.busy || action.pending,
    available = props.actions.some(
      (item) => item.id === "approve" && item.available,
    );
  const note = String(props.draft.note ?? "");
  return (
    <section aria-label="审核与结果">
      <h3>审核与结果</h3>
      <p>
        {String(props.projection.data.title)} ·{" "}
        {stateLabels[props.projection.state] ?? props.projection.state}
      </p>
      <p>{String(props.projection.data.body ?? "")}</p>
      {props.projection.data.review_note && (
        <p>审核意见：{String(props.projection.data.review_note)}</p>
      )}
      <label>
        审核意见
        <textarea
          aria-label="审核意见"
          value={note}
          maxLength={1000}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, note: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !available || !note.trim()}
        onClick={() =>
          void action.run(() => props.execute("approve", { note }))
        }
      >
        审核通过
      </button>
      {!available && (
        <p>仅具有本项目范围审核权限且不是作者的办理人，可审核待审记录。</p>
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked}
        onClick={() => void action.run(() => props.refresh())}
      >
        重新读取结果
      </button>
      {action.error && <p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
export const stepComponents: WorkflowStepComponents = {
  Picker,
  Editor,
  Review,
};
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/tests/workflow.test.mjs

```javascript
/** Real isolated HTTP/SQLite tests. The central decision endpoint below is a synthetic stub,
 * not a substitute for registration, real grants, host UI, or production browser acceptance. */
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import http from "node:http";
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import {
  createService,
  manifest,
  registration,
  moduleGraph,
} from "../backend/server.mjs";
import { createObservationClient } from "../sdk/node/observation.mjs";
import { compile, createModuleServer } from "../sdk/node/runtime.mjs";
import { createAtomBindings } from "../sdk/node/atoms.mjs";
import {
  assertTransition,
  assertAtomResult,
} from "../sdk/node/graph-testing.mjs";

const M = manifest.module.id;
const token = "synthetic-test-service-credential-32";
const projects = [
  { project_id: "P", organization_id: "O", title: "合成 P" },
  { project_id: "Q", organization_id: "Z", title: "合成 Q" },
];
const actor = (id) => ({
  id,
  name: id,
  role: "member",
  roles: ["member"],
  organization_id: "O",
  team_ids: [],
  authorization_revision: 1,
});
const id = () => randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
async function listen(server) {
  await new Promise((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
  return `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
}
async function fixture(t) {
  const directory = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), "module-kit-node-"));
  const tickets = new Map(),
    seen = [],
    revoked = new Set();
  let unavailable = false,
    denyCommit = false;
  const central = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
    let body = "";
    for await (const chunk of req) body += chunk;
    const input = JSON.parse(body),
      claims = tickets.get(req.headers["x-platform-access"]);
    if (req.url === "/api/v1/runtime-observations/probe") {
      res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
      res.end(JSON.stringify({ accepted: true, nonce: input.nonce }));
      return;
    }
    seen.push(input);
    let reason = "allowed";
    if (unavailable) {
      res.writeHead(503);
      res.end("{}");
      return;
    }
    if (!claims || claims.expired) reason = "access_ticket_expired";
    else if (
      claims.requestId !== input.request_id ||
      claims.operation !== input.operation ||
      claims.permission !== input.permission
    )
      reason = "access_ticket_permission_mismatch";
    else if (revoked.has(claims.actor)) reason = "access_authorization_changed";
    else if (
      input.facts.project_id !== "P" ||
      input.facts.organization_id !== "O" ||
      claims.actor === "outsider"
    )
      reason = "grant_scope_mismatch";
    else if (
      ["edit", "submit"].some(
        (suffix) => input.permission === `${M}.${suffix}`,
      ) &&
      input.facts.author_id !== claims.actor
    )
      reason = "actor_relation_mismatch";
    else if (
      input.permission === `${M}.approve` &&
      input.facts.author_id === claims.actor
    )
      reason = "separation_of_duties";
    else if (input.permission === `${M}.approve` && claims.actor !== "reviewer")
      reason = "grant_scope_mismatch";
    else if (
      input.permission !== `${M}.read` &&
      claims.actor === "reviewer" &&
      input.permission !== `${M}.approve`
    )
      reason = "grant_scope_mismatch";
    else if (
      denyCommit &&
      input.permission === `${M}.create` &&
      seen.filter((item) => item.request_id === input.request_id).length > 1
    )
      reason = "access_authorization_changed";
    res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
    res.end(
      JSON.stringify({
        allowed: reason === "allowed",
        reason,
        policy_revision: reason.startsWith("access_") ? 0 : 1,
      }),
    );
  });
  const centralUrl = await listen(central),
    filename = path.join(directory, "records.sqlite");
  let service = createService({
      token,
      callbackUrl: centralUrl + "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
      filename,
      projects,
    }),
    url = await listen(service.server);
  const makeBody = (action, input, who = "author") => ({
    protocol_version: "1.1",
    request_id: id(),
    capability_id: `${M}.${action}`,
    capability_version: "1.0.0",
    input,
    context: { workspace_id: "synthetic-workspace", actor: actor(who) },
  });
  async function request(body, operation = "invoke", options = {}) {
    const ticket = "synthetic-ticket-" + id();
    const suffix = body.capability_id?.slice(M.length + 1);
    tickets.set(ticket, {
      requestId: body.request_id,
      operation,
      permission: `${M}.${operation === "invoke" && suffix !== "list" ? suffix : "read"}`,
      actor: body.context.actor.id,
      expired: options.expired,
    });
    const headers = {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      ...(!options.noTicket ? { "X-Platform-Access": ticket } : {}),
    };
    const result = await fetch(
      url +
        "/platform/v1/" +
        (operation === "invoke" ? "invoke" : `workflow/${operation}`),
      { method: "POST", headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) },
    );
    return { status: result.status, body: await result.json(), ticket };
  }
  const invoke = (action, input, who = "author", options = {}) =>
    request(makeBody(action, input, who), "invoke", options);
  const flow = (operation, input, who = "author", options = {}) =>
    request(
      {
        protocol_version: "1.0",
        request_id: id(),
        context: { actor: actor(who) },
        input,
      },
      operation,
      options,
    );
  const create = (title = "合成记录") =>
    invoke("create", { command: { project_id: "P", title, body: "内容" } });
  t.after(async () => {
    await service.close();
    await new Promise((resolve) => central.close(resolve));
    rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });
  return {
    get url() {
      return url;
    },
    filename,
    tickets,
    seen,
    revoked,
    makeBody,
    request,
    invoke,
    flow,
    create,
    setUnavailable: (value) => {
      unavailable = value;
    },
    setDenyCommit: (value) => {
      denyCommit = value;
    },
    restart: async () => {
      await service.close();
      service = createService({
        token,
        callbackUrl: centralUrl + "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        filename,
        projects,
      });
      url = await listen(service.server);
    },
  };
}

test("health and manifest use real HTTP and require the private service bearer", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (const endpoint of ["health", "manifest"]) {
    assert.equal((await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/" + endpoint)).status, 401);
    const response = await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/" + endpoint, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    });
    assert.equal(response.status, 200);
    const data = await response.json();
    assert.equal(
      endpoint === "health" ? data.status : data.protocol_version,
      endpoint === "health" ? "ok" : "1.2",
    );
  }
});

test("registration checks both complete contracts and actual bindings without business writes", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    headers = {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
  assert.equal((await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/registration")).status, 401);
  assert.equal((await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/module-graph")).status, 401);
  assert.deepEqual(
    await (
      await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/module-graph", { headers })
    ).json(),
    moduleGraph,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    await (
      await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/registration", { headers })
    ).json(),
    registration,
  );
  const offer = {
    registration_version: "1.1",
    nonce: "a".repeat(32),
    contract_digest: "b".repeat(64),
    platform_features: [
      "invoke-1.1",
      "access-1.0",
      "shared-hosts-1.0",
      "registration-1.0",
      "registration-1.1",
      "module-graph-1.0",
      "flow-exports-1.0",
      "runtime-observation-1.0",
    ],
    manifest,
    registration,
    runtime_probe: { ticket: "c".repeat(64), nonce: "d".repeat(32) },
  };
  async function confirm(body) {
    return fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/handshake", {
      method: "POST",
      headers,
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  }
  const accepted = await confirm(offer);
  assert.equal(accepted.status, 200);
  const answer = await accepted.json();
  assert.equal(answer.accepted, true);
  assert.deepEqual(
    answer.capability_ids,
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => cap.id).sort(),
  );
  for (const field of ["manifest", "registration", "platform_features"])
    assert.equal((await confirm({ ...offer, [field]: [] })).status, 422);
  assert.equal((await confirm({})).status, 422);
  const database = new DatabaseSync(f.filename, { readOnly: true });
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM records").get().n,
    0,
  );
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM kit_receipts").get().n,
    0,
  );
  database.close();
  assert.equal(f.seen.length, 0);
});
test("atomic startup binding rejects missing functions and never runs business handlers", () => {
  let calls = 0;
  const handlers = Object.fromEntries(
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => [
      cap.id,
      () => {
        calls++;
      },
    ]),
  );
  const bindings = createAtomBindings({
    graph: moduleGraph,
    manifest,
    handlers,
  });
  assert.equal(
    bindings.forCapability(manifest.capabilities[0].id).kind,
    "backend",
  );
  const copy = bindings.document;
  copy.atoms.length = 0;
  assert.ok(bindings.document.atoms.length > 0);
  assert.throws(() =>
    createAtomBindings({ graph: moduleGraph, manifest, handlers: {} }),
  );
  assert.equal(calls, 0);
});

      })
    ).status,
    422,
  );

      await f.invoke("create", {

          project_id: "P",
          organization_id: "FORGED",

          body: "",
        },
      })
    ).status,

  );

    command: { project_id: "P", title: "x", body: "" },
  });

  const created = await f.create();

});
test("missing, expired and unavailable central decisions fail closed even for empty lists", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (const options of [{ noTicket: true }, { expired: true }])
    assert.equal(
      (await f.flow("objects.list", {}, "author", options)).status,
      403,
    );
  f.setUnavailable(true);
  assert.equal((await f.create()).status, 403);
  assert.equal((await f.flow("objects.list", {})).status, 403);
  f.setUnavailable(false);
  assert.deepEqual((await f.flow("objects.list", {})).body.data.objects, []);
});
test("draft -> submitted -> approved requires scoped non-author reviewer; formal states persist", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    created = await f.create(),
    object_id = created.body.data.projection.object_ref.object_id;
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke(
        "edit",
        {
          object_id,
          expected_revision: 1,
          command: { title: "他人编辑", body: "" },
        },
        "reviewer",
      )
    ).status,
    403,
  );
  for (const name of ["constructor", "toString", "__proto__"]) {
    assert.equal(
      (
        await f.invoke("submit", {
          object_id,
          expected_revision: 1,
          command: JSON.parse(`{"${name}":3}`),
        })
      ).status,
      422,
    );
  }
  const submitted = await f.invoke("submit", {
    object_id,
    expected_revision: 1,
    command: {},
  });
  assert.equal(submitted.body.data.projection.state, "submitted");
  assertTransition(
    moduleGraph,
    `${M}.record-lifecycle`,
    created.body.data.projection,
    "submit",
    submitted.body.data.projection,
  );
  const atom = moduleGraph.atoms.find(
    (item) => item.capability?.id === `${M}.submit`,
  );
  assertAtomResult(
    moduleGraph,
    atom.id,
    { object_id, expected_revision: 1, command: {} },
    submitted.body.data,
    (schema, value) => assert.ok(compile(schema)(value)),
    manifest,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke("approve", {
        object_id,
        expected_revision: 2,
        command: { note: "自审" },
      })
    ).status,
    403,
  );
  const approved = await f.invoke(
    "approve",
    { object_id, expected_revision: 2, command: { note: "合成审核通过" } },
    "reviewer",
  );
  assert.equal(approved.status, 200);
  assert.equal(approved.body.data.projection.revision, 3);
  assert.equal(approved.body.data.projection.state, "approved");
  assertTransition(
    moduleGraph,
    `${M}.record-lifecycle`,
    submitted.body.data.projection,
    "approve",
    approved.body.data.projection,
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    assertTransition(
      moduleGraph,
      `${M}.record-lifecycle`,
      created.body.data.projection,
      "approve",
      approved.body.data.projection,
    ),
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke("edit", {
        object_id,
        expected_revision: 3,
        command: { title: "不应修改", body: "" },
      })
    ).status,
    409,
  );
  await f.restart();
  const read = await f.flow(
    "objects.read",
    { object_ref: { object_type: M + ".record", object_id } },
    "reviewer",
  );
  assert.equal(read.body.data.projection.data.review_note, "合成审核通过");
});
test("atomic idempotency survives restart, reauthorizes replay, and never persists tickets", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    body = f.makeBody("create", {
      command: { project_id: "P", title: "幂等", body: "" },
    });
  const first = await f.request(body);
  await f.restart();
  const replay = await f.request(body);
  assert.deepEqual(replay.body, first.body);
  const conflict = structuredClone(body);
  conflict.input.command.title = "改变";
  assert.equal((await f.request(conflict)).status, 409);
  const database = new DatabaseSync(f.filename, { readOnly: true });
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM records").get().n,
    1,
  );
  const receipts = database.prepare("SELECT * FROM kit_receipts").all();
  database.close();
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(receipts).includes(first.ticket));
  assert.ok(!readFileSync(f.filename).includes(Buffer.from(first.ticket)));
  f.revoked.add("author");
  assert.equal((await f.request(body)).status, 403);
});

test("read and list are scoped before pagination, and cursors bind actor and revision snapshot", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (let n = 0; n < 3; n++)
    assert.equal((await f.create("记录" + n)).status, 200);
  const first = await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2 });
  assert.equal(first.body.data.objects.length, 2);
  assert.ok(first.body.data.next_cursor);
  const cursor = first.body.data.next_cursor,
    object = first.body.data.objects[0];
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor })).body.data.objects
      .length,
    1,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor }, "reviewer")).status,
    409,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", {}, "outsider")).body.data.objects,
    [],
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke(
        "read",
        { object_id: object.object_ref.object_id },
        "outsider",
      )
    ).status,
    403,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor: cursor + "bad" })).status,
    422,
  );
  await f.invoke("edit", {
    object_id: object.object_ref.object_id,
    expected_revision: 1,
    command: { title: "更新", body: "" },
  });
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor })).status,
    409,
  );
  assert.ok(
    first.body.data.objects.every((item) => !Object.hasOwn(item.data, "body")),
  );
});

  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (const operation of ["objects.detail", "uploads.create", "jobs.status"])
    assert.equal((await f.flow(operation, {})).status, 501);
  assert.throws(() => compile({ type: "object", allOf: [] }), /Schema/);

      compile({ type: "object", properties: { value: { $ref: "#/$defs/a" } } }),

test("schema subset never interprets prototype names as validators or ignores nested extra-field schemas", () => {
  const closed = compile({
    type: "object",
    properties: {},
    additionalProperties: false,
  });
  for (const name of ["constructor", "toString", "__proto__"])
    assert.equal(closed(JSON.parse(`{"${name}":3}`)), false);
  assert.throws(
    () => compile({ type: "object", additionalProperties: { type: "string" } }),
    /additionalProperties/,
  );
  const explicit = compile(
    JSON.parse(
      '{"type":"object","properties":{"__proto__":{"type":"string"}},"additionalProperties":false}',
    ),
  );
  assert.equal(explicit(JSON.parse('{"__proto__":"data"}')), true);
  assert.equal(explicit(JSON.parse('{"__proto__":3}')), false);
  assert.equal(Object.prototype.polluted, undefined);
});

test("observation delivery retries stable identities and isolates collector failures", async () => {
  const batches = [];
  const observer = createObservationClient({
    callbackUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:9999/api/v1/runtime-observations/events",
    graph: moduleGraph,
    fetchImpl: async (url, options) => {
      batches.push(JSON.parse(options.body));
      throw new Error("offline");
    },
  });
  const session = observer.session({
    ticket: "a".repeat(64),
    requestId: "b".repeat(32),
    atomId: moduleGraph.atoms[0].id,
  });
  session.mark("started");
  const result = { value: 7 };
  session.mark("succeeded");
  await observer.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(result, { value: 7 });
  assert.deepEqual(session.status(), { sequence: 2, dropped: 2 });
  assert.equal(batches.length, 4);
  assert.ok(
    batches.every((batch) =>
      batch.events.every((event) => !Object.hasOwn(event, "input")),
    ),
  );
  const absent = observer.session({
    requestId: "c".repeat(32),
    atomId: moduleGraph.atoms[0].id,
  });
  absent.mark("started");
  await observer.flush();
  assert.equal(batches.length, 4);
});

  };
  const server = createModuleServer({
    manifest: m,

  const url = await listen(server);
  t.after(() => new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve)));
  const body = {

    capability_version: cap.version,
    input: { text: "合成文字" },
    context: { workspace_id: "test", actor: actor("member") },
  };
  async function send(value) {
    return fetch(url + "/platform/v1/invoke", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(value),
    });
  }
  const accepted = await send(body);
  assert.equal(accepted.status, 200);
  assert.equal((await accepted.json()).data.text, "合成文字");
  body.context.actor.role = "researcher";
  body.context.actor.roles = ["researcher"];
  assert.equal((await send(body)).status, 403);
});
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/frontend/atoms.ts

```typescript
/** Stable UI identities shared by page, conversation and the runtime graph.
 * These definitions describe component contracts; authorization and business
 * state transitions remain server responsibilities.
 */
export type AtomSchema = Record<string, unknown>;
export type AtomicHost = {
  kind: "interaction" | "workflow_step";
  id: string;
  version: string;
  step_id?: string;
};
export type AtomicEvent = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  input_schema: AtomSchema;
  output_schema: AtomSchema;
  effect: string;
  state_effect: string;
  capability?: { id: string; version: string };
};
export type FrontendAtom = {
  id: string;
  kind: "frontend";
  title: string;
  description: string;
  host: AtomicHost;
  input_schema: AtomSchema;
  output_schema: AtomSchema;
  state_schema: AtomSchema;
  initial_state: Record<string, unknown>;
  events: AtomicEvent[];
};
export type AtomNodeMapping = {
  atom_id: string;
  node_id: string;
  role: "handles" | "shows" | "reads" | "writes";
  event_id?: string;
};

/** Bind concrete components once. No downloaded code or implicit name matching. */
export function registerAtomicComponents<T>(
  definitions: FrontendAtom[],
  components: Record<string, T>,
) {
  const table = new Map<string, { definition: FrontendAtom; component: T }>();
  for (const definition of definitions) {
    if (table.has(definition.id) || !Object.hasOwn(components, definition.id)) {
      throw new Error("组件登记重复，或没有对应的实际组件。");
    }
    table.set(definition.id, {
      definition: structuredClone(definition),
      component: components[definition.id],
    });
  }
  if (table.size !== Object.keys(components).length) {
    throw new Error("实际组件中存在未登记项。");
  }
  return {
    get(id: string) {
      const entry = table.get(id);
      if (!entry) throw new Error("组件未登记。");
      return {
        definition: structuredClone(entry.definition),
        component: entry.component,
      };
    },
    list() {
      return [...table.values()].map((entry) =>
        structuredClone(entry.definition),
      );
    },
  };
}

/** A host resolves a definition by its full binding, never by a translated title. */
export function atomForHost(atoms: FrontendAtom[], host: AtomicHost) {
  const matches = atoms.filter(
    (atom) =>
      atom.host.kind === host.kind &&
      atom.host.id === host.id &&
      atom.host.version === host.version &&
      atom.host.step_id === host.step_id,
  );
  if (matches.length > 1) throw new Error("同一宿主重复登记。");
  return matches[0] ?? null;
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/README.md

```markdown
# Node 模块 SDK v1

这是可随模块交付的服务端代码，要求 Node.js 24。生成器把本目录复制到生成包的 `sdk/node/`。不依赖 npm 安装，不导入主框架内部 Python，也不包含浏览器 SDK。

稳定边界：

测试辅助文件 `graph-testing.mjs` 提供 `assertTransition(graph, machineId, before, event, after)` 与 `assertAtomResult(graph, atomId, inputs, output, validate)`。先运行真实业务函数，再用实际结果核对状态边与原子输入输出。后端原子另以第六个参数传入实际 manifest，按能力 ID 和版本读取 Schema。`validate(schema, value)` 校验失败时应抛出异常。模板的 HTTP/SQLite 测试已把真实提交、审核结果与状态图核对，权限及数据库结果仍分别断言。

| 接口 | 职责 |
| --- | --- |
| `createModuleServer({manifest, registration, token, handlers, workflowHandlers})` | 固定 HTTP 路径、恒定时间 Bearer 校验、64 KiB 输入、invoke 1.1 / workflow 1.0 包络、能力输入输出校验和安全错误；通过真实函数表分派 |
| `createRegistration(...)` | 启动时检查函数完整覆盖；握手时核对平台支持与完整声明后确认。不会调用业务函数 |
| `createAccessClient({callbackUrl, timeoutMs, fetchImpl})` | 向服务端配置的固定中央地址请求授权；票据缺失、超时、重定向、异常或无效回复均拒绝 |
| `Store.transaction(callback)` | 单连接串行任务及 SQLite 原子提交；业务对象和成功回执在同一事务中保存 |
| `Store.receipt/saveReceipt` | 请求编号绑定整个请求指纹，不包含传输头；重放前由业务重新授权 |
| `Store.cursor/offset` | HMAC 游标与调用人、授权修订、目录快照绑定；游标本身不授予权限 |

`invoke({body,ticket})` 返回 `{summary,data}`。`workflow({body,operation,ticket})` 返回适配器 `data`。`ticket` 只在当前内存调用栈使用，不返回、记录、写入仓储或传给前端。`facts` 必须来自服务端仓储；本 SDK 不会把输入自动变成可信事实。

`compile` 是明确封闭的 Schema 子集，支持本模板所需的 type、properties、required、布尔形式 additionalProperties、items、长度/数值范围、uniqueItems、pattern、enum、const 及说明字段。它不是完整 JSON Schema 引擎；Schema 形式的 additionalProperties、`$ref`、组合条件、format 等未支持内容在启动时拒绝。字段只按对象自身属性匹配，constructor、toString 和 __proto__ 不具有特殊验证能力。业务需要完整 Draft 2020-12 时，应以锁定版本的成熟验证库替换此适配点，并保留测试；不能静默忽略不认识的约束。

当前事务参考只面向一个 Node 进程和本地 SQLite。不要把持锁期间的中央回调扩展成长计算或外部写入；分布式副作用需要 outbox/inbox、领域幂等和恢复设计。本 SDK 不提供登录、角色引擎、游客、SSO、通知、长期委派、文件存储、长任务或聊天分支；这些能力不能通过添加同名配置字段声称实现。

SDK 升级随包显式发布，不从网络动态加载代码。已有独立服务可以只复用 `access-client.mjs` 和固定传输边界，保留原数据库与业务事务；不要求迁移到参考 `Store`。
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/access-client.mjs

```javascript
/** Trusted server-side client. Never import this into a browser bundle. */
export function createAccessClient({
  callbackUrl,
  timeoutMs = 4000,
  fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
}) {
  const endpoint = new URL(callbackUrl);
  if (
    !["http:", "https:"].includes(endpoint.protocol) ||
    endpoint.username ||
    endpoint.password ||
    endpoint.hash ||
    endpoint.search ||
    endpoint.pathname !== "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid configured access-decision endpoint");
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1 || timeoutMs > 10000) {
    throw new Error("Access decision timeout must be 1-10000 milliseconds");
  }
  // callbackUrl comes exclusively from deployment configuration. No URL, actor,
  // role, token destination or fetch options are accepted from invocation input.
  return async function decideAccess({
    ticket,
    requestId,
    operation,
    permission,
    facts,
  }) {
    const deny = (reason) => ({ allowed: false, reason, policy_revision: 0 });
    if (
      typeof ticket !== "string" ||
      ticket.length === 0 ||
      ticket.length > 12288
    )
      return deny("access_ticket_invalid");
    let body;
    try {
      body = JSON.stringify({
        request_id: requestId,
        operation,
        permission,
        facts,
      });
    } catch {
      return deny("access_decision_contract");
    }
    if (Buffer.byteLength(body, "utf8") > 16384)
      return deny("access_decision_size");
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
    try {
      const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {
        method: "POST",
        redirect: "error",
        signal: controller.signal,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          "X-Platform-Access": ticket,
        },
        body,
      });
      if (!response.ok) return deny("access_decision_unavailable");
      // Read bounded bytes rather than response.json() on an unbounded body.
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const chunks = [];
      let size = 0;
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > 4096) {
          await reader.cancel();
          return deny("access_decision_response_size");
        }
        chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
      }
      const result = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
      if (
        typeof result.allowed !== "boolean" ||
        typeof result.reason !== "string" ||
        !Number.isInteger(result.policy_revision) ||
        result.policy_revision < 0 ||
        (result.allowed &&
          (result.reason !== "allowed" || result.policy_revision < 1))
      )
        return deny("access_decision_invalid");
      return {
        allowed: result.allowed,
        reason: result.reason,
        policy_revision: result.policy_revision,
      };
    } catch {
      return deny("access_decision_unavailable");
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/atoms.mjs

```javascript
/** Associate actual dispatch functions with stable atom identities.
 * Structural and host validation belongs to module_kit check/admission. This
 * startup binding check never invokes a handler or grants user permissions.
 */
export function createAtomBindings({ graph, manifest, handlers }) {
  const need = (value) => {
    if (!value)
      throw Object.assign(new Error("原子登记与实际函数绑定不一致。"), {
        code: "atomic_binding",
        status: 422,
      });
  };
  need(
    graph?.graph_version === "1.0" &&
      graph.module_id === manifest.module.id &&
      graph.module_version === manifest.module.version,
  );
  const capabilities = new Map(
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => [cap.id, cap]),
  );
  const backends = graph.atoms.filter((atom) => atom.kind === "backend");
  const bindings = new Map(
    backends.map((atom) => [atom.capability.id, structuredClone(atom)]),
  );
  need(
    bindings.size === backends.length &&
      bindings.size === capabilities.size &&
      Object.keys(handlers).length === capabilities.size,
  );
  for (const [id, capability] of capabilities) {
    need(
      Object.hasOwn(handlers, id) &&
        typeof handlers[id] === "function" &&
        bindings.get(id)?.capability.version === capability.version,
    );
  }
  const document = structuredClone(graph);
  return Object.freeze({
    get document() {
      return structuredClone(document);
    },
    forCapability(id) {
      need(bindings.has(id));
      return structuredClone(bindings.get(id));
    },
  });
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/graph-testing.mjs

```javascript
/** Assertions over actual handler results; no transition is executed here. */
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

export function assertTransition(graph, machineId, before, event, after) {
  const machines = graph.state_machines.filter((item) => item.id === machineId);
  assert.equal(machines.length, 1, `Expected one state machine: ${machineId}`);
  assert.deepEqual(
    before.object_ref,
    after.object_ref,
    "The object changed between reads",
  );
  assert.ok(
    machines[0].transitions.some(
      (edge) =>
        edge.from === before.state &&
        edge.event === event &&
        edge.to === after.state,
    ),
    `Observed transition is absent from the graph: ${before.state} -> ${event} -> ${after.state}`,
  );
}

/** Supply the same Schema validator used by the application or test harness. */
export function assertAtomResult(
  graph,
  atomId,
  inputs,
  output,
  validate,
  manifest = null,
) {
  const atoms = graph.atoms.filter((item) => item.id === atomId);
  assert.equal(atoms.length, 1, `Expected one atom: ${atomId}`);
  let contract = atoms[0];
  if (contract.kind === "backend") {
    const matching = (manifest?.capabilities || []).filter(
      (capability) =>
        capability.id === contract.capability.id &&
        capability.version === contract.capability.version,
    );
    assert.equal(
      matching.length,
      1,
      "Backend atom requires its actual manifest capability",
    );
    contract = matching[0];
  }
  validate(contract.input_schema, inputs);
  validate(contract.output_schema, output);
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/observation.mjs

```javascript
/** Server-side observation queue. Business handlers never wait for delivery. */
export function createObservationClient({
  callbackUrl,
  graph,
  fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
  timeoutMs = 1000,
}) {
  const endpoint = new URL(callbackUrl);
  if (
    !["http:", "https:"].includes(endpoint.protocol) ||
    endpoint.username ||
    endpoint.password ||
    endpoint.hash ||
    endpoint.search ||
    endpoint.pathname !== "/api/v1/runtime-observations/events"
  )
    throw new Error("Invalid configured observation endpoint");
  if (!Number.isInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1 || timeoutMs > 2000)
    throw new Error(
      "Observation timeout must be between 1 and 2000 milliseconds",
    );
  const definition = structuredClone(graph);
  const active = new Set();
  let pending = 0;

  /** Keep a bounded queue and retry the same event identity at most once. */
  function deliver(ticket, batch, lost) {
    if (pending >= 64) {
      lost();
      return;
    }
    pending += 1;
    const task = (async () => {
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt += 1) {
        try {
          const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {
            method: "POST",
            redirect: "error",
            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
            headers: {
              "Content-Type": "application/json",
              "X-DuoFlow-Observation": ticket,
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(batch),
          });
          const ok = response.ok;
          await response.body?.cancel();
          if (ok) return;
          if (response.status < 500) break;
        } catch {
          /* Delivery problems never change a business result. */
        }
      }
      lost();
    })().finally(() => {
      pending -= 1;
      active.delete(task);
    });
    active.add(task);
  }

  return {
    /** Admission uses the configured destination and a one-time challenge. */
    async probe(challenge) {
      if (
        !challenge ||
        !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(challenge.ticket) ||
        !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(challenge.nonce)
      )
        return false;
      try {
        const url = new URL(endpoint);
        url.pathname = "/api/v1/runtime-observations/probe";
        const response = await fetchImpl(url, {
          method: "POST",
          redirect: "error",
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "X-DuoFlow-Observation": challenge.ticket,
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            runtime_version: "1.0",
            nonce: challenge.nonce,
          }),
        });
        if (!response.ok) {
          await response.body?.cancel();
          return false;
        }
        const reader = response.body.getReader();
        let data = "",
          size = 0;
        while (true) {
          const { value, done } = await reader.read();
          if (done) break;
          size += value.byteLength;
          if (size > 1024) {
            await reader.cancel();
            return false;
          }
          data += new TextDecoder().decode(value);
        }
        const result = JSON.parse(data);
        return result.accepted === true && result.nonce === challenge.nonce;
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    },
    /** A request owns its sequence and correlation; tickets stay in memory. */
    session({ ticket, requestId, atomId }) {
      let sequence = 0,
        dropped = 0;
      const valid =
        typeof ticket === "string" &&
        /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(ticket) &&
        /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(requestId);
      const emit = (phase, state) => {
        if (!valid) return;
        if (sequence >= 256) {
          dropped = Math.min(256, dropped + 1);
          return;
        }
        const event = {
          sequence: ++sequence,
          atom_id: atomId,
          call_id: requestId,
          parent_call_id: null,
          phase,
        };
        if (state) event.state = structuredClone(state);
        deliver(
          ticket,
          {
            runtime_version: "1.0",
            request_id: requestId,
            graph_version: definition.version,
            dropped,
            events: [event],
          },
          () => {
            dropped = Math.min(256, dropped + 1);
          },
        );
      };
      return {
        mark: (phase) => {
          if (["started", "succeeded", "failed"].includes(phase)) emit(phase);
        },
        /** Call only after the module has committed or read this actual projection. */
        state(machineId, projection) {
          if (
            !projection?.object_ref ||
            !Number.isSafeInteger(projection.revision)
          )
            return;
          emit("state", {
            machine_id: machineId,
            value: projection.state,
            revision: projection.revision,
            object_ref: projection.object_ref,
          });
        },
        status: () => ({ sequence, dropped }),
      };
    },
    /** Optional shutdown/test hook; ordinary requests do not await it. */
    flush: () => Promise.allSettled([...active]),
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/registration.mjs

```javascript
/** Bilateral registration v1. No business handler is executed by a handshake.
 * Transport authentication belongs to runtime.mjs; this file only compares
 * contracts and checks the same handler maps that runtime dispatches through.
 */
import { createAtomBindings } from "./atoms.mjs";

const plain = (value) =>
  value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const sorted = (value) =>
  Array.isArray(value)
    ? value.map(sorted)
    : plain(value)
      ? Object.fromEntries(
          Object.keys(value)
            .sort()
            .map((key) => [key, sorted(value[key])]),
        )
      : value;
const equal = (left, right) =>
  JSON.stringify(sorted(left)) === JSON.stringify(sorted(right));
function need(ok) {
  if (!ok)
    throw Object.assign(
      new Error("注册确认未通过，请检查协议、声明与接口绑定。"),
      { code: "registration_contract", status: 422 },
    );
}

/** Fail at service startup on missing/extra handlers or unsupported schemas. */
export function createRegistration({
  manifest,
  registration,
  handlers,
  workflowHandlers = {},
  compile,
  graph = null,
  runtimeProbe = null,
}) {
  need(
    ["1.0", "1.1"].includes(registration?.registration_version) &&
      manifest.protocol_version === "1.2",
  );
  need(
    registration.module_id === manifest.module.id &&
      registration.module_version === manifest.module.version,
  );
  const ids = manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => cap.id).sort();
  const modern = registration.registration_version === "1.1";
  if (modern) {
    need(
      equal(graph, registration.module_graph) &&
        typeof runtimeProbe === "function",
    );
    createAtomBindings({ graph, manifest, handlers });
  }
  need(
    equal(Object.keys(handlers).sort(), ids) &&
      Object.values(handlers).every((fn) => typeof fn === "function"),
  );
  need(
    equal(
      Object.keys(workflowHandlers).sort(),
      registration.workflow_operations,
    ) &&
      Object.values(workflowHandlers).every((fn) => typeof fn === "function"),
  );
  for (const capability of manifest.capabilities) {
    compile(capability.input_schema);
    compile(capability.output_schema);
  }
  // Freeze a private copy: later caller mutation cannot change accepted contracts.
  const localManifest = structuredClone(manifest),
    localRegistration = structuredClone(registration);
  return {
    get document() {
      return structuredClone(localRegistration);
    },
    confirm(offer) {
      need(
        plain(offer) &&
          equal(
            Object.keys(offer).sort(),
            [
              "registration_version",
              "nonce",
              "contract_digest",
              "platform_features",
              "manifest",
              "registration",
              ...(modern ? ["runtime_probe"] : []),
            ].sort(),
          ),
      );
      need(
        offer.registration_version === localRegistration.registration_version &&
          /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(offer.nonce) &&
          /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(offer.contract_digest),
      );
      const required = ["invoke-1.1", "shared-hosts-1.0", "registration-1.0"];
      if (modern)
        required.push(
          "registration-1.1",
          "module-graph-1.0",
          "flow-exports-1.0",
          "runtime-observation-1.0",
        );
      if (localRegistration.permissions.mode === "platform_access")
        required.push("access-1.0");
      need(
        Array.isArray(offer.platform_features) &&
          required.every((feature) =>
            offer.platform_features.includes(feature),
          ),
      );
      need(
        equal(offer.manifest, localManifest) &&
          equal(offer.registration, localRegistration),
      );
      const answer = {
        registration_version: localRegistration.registration_version,
        nonce: offer.nonce,
        contract_digest: offer.contract_digest,
        accepted: true,
        checks: {
          platform_contract: true,
          local_manifest: true,
          local_registration: true,
          handlers: true,
          schemas: true,
        },
        capability_ids: [...ids],
        workflow_operations: [...localRegistration.workflow_operations],
      };
      if (!modern) return answer;
      return Promise.resolve(runtimeProbe(offer.runtime_probe)).then(
        (accepted) => {
          need(accepted === true);
          return {
            ...answer,
            checks: {
              ...answer.checks,
              atomic_bindings: true,
              runtime_callback: true,
            },
          };
        },
      );
    },
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/runtime.mjs

```javascript
/** Portable module-kit transport v1. Server only; no login, RBAC or browser credentials. */
import http from "node:http";
import { timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { createRegistration } from "./registration.mjs";
import { createAtomBindings } from "./atoms.mjs";

export function fail(code, message, status = 422) {
  return Object.assign(new Error(message), { code, status });
}
export function requireThat(condition, code, message, status = 422) {
  if (!condition) throw fail(code, message, status);
}
export const canonical = (value) => JSON.stringify(sort(value));
function sort(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(sort);
  if (value && typeof value === "object")
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.keys(value)
        .sort()
        .map((key) => [key, sort(value[key])]),
    );
  return value;
}
const plain = (value) =>
  value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const keywords = new Set([
  "type",
  "properties",
  "required",
  "additionalProperties",
  "items",
  "minItems",
  "maxItems",
  "uniqueItems",
  "minLength",
  "maxLength",
  "minimum",
  "maximum",
  "pattern",
  "enum",
  "const",
  "title",
  "description",
  "default",
  "$schema",
]);
```
