```javascript
/** Deliberately closed JSON Schema subset, not an implementation of arbitrary JSON Schema.
 * Unknown validation keywords fail at startup; install a full validator before expanding it. */
export function compile(schema) {
  requireThat(
    plain(schema) && Object.keys(schema).every((key) => keywords.has(key)),
    "schema_unsupported",
    "SDK 不支持此 Schema 关键字。",
    500,
  );
  requireThat(
    schema.additionalProperties === undefined ||
      typeof schema.additionalProperties === "boolean",
    "schema_unsupported",
    "SDK 的 additionalProperties 只支持布尔值。",
    500,
  );
  requireThat(
    schema.properties === undefined || plain(schema.properties),
    "schema_unsupported",
    "Schema properties 必须是对象。",
    500,
  );
  const types = Array.isArray(schema.type) ? schema.type : [schema.type];
  requireThat(
    types.every((type) =>
      [
        "object",
        "array",
        "string",
        "integer",
        "number",
        "boolean",
        "null",
      ].includes(type),
    ),
    "schema_unsupported",
    "Schema 必须显式声明受支持类型。",
    500,
  );
  const children = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(schema.properties || {}).map(([key, value]) => [
      key,
      compile(value),
    ]),
  );
  const child = schema.items ? compile(schema.items) : null;
  const pattern = schema.pattern ? new RegExp(schema.pattern, "u") : null;
  return (value) => {
    const type =
      value === null ? "null" : Array.isArray(value) ? "array" : typeof value;
    if (
      !types.includes(type) &&
      !(types.includes("integer") && Number.isSafeInteger(value))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      typeof value === "number" &&
      (!Number.isFinite(value) ||
        (schema.minimum !== undefined && value < schema.minimum) ||
        (schema.maximum !== undefined && value > schema.maximum))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      Object.hasOwn(schema, "const") &&
      canonical(schema.const) !== canonical(value)
    )
      return false;
    if (
      schema.enum &&
      !schema.enum.some((item) => canonical(item) === canonical(value))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      typeof value === "string" &&
      ((schema.minLength !== undefined &&
        [...value].length < schema.minLength) ||
        (schema.maxLength !== undefined &&
          [...value].length > schema.maxLength) ||
        (pattern && !pattern.test(value)))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      Array.isArray(value) &&
      ((schema.minItems !== undefined && value.length < schema.minItems) ||
        (schema.maxItems !== undefined && value.length > schema.maxItems) ||
        (schema.uniqueItems &&
          new Set(value.map(canonical)).size !== value.length) ||
        (child && !value.every(child)))
    )
      return false;
    if (plain(value)) {
      if ((schema.required || []).some((key) => !Object.hasOwn(value, key)))
        return false;
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        // Only own properties are declared fields. constructor/toString must not
        // call Object.prototype functions as validators; __proto__ remains data.
        if (
          Object.hasOwn(children, key)
            ? !children[key](item)
            : schema.additionalProperties === false
        )
          return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  };
}
export const objectSchema = (properties) => ({
  type: "object",
  properties,
  required: Object.keys(properties),
  additionalProperties: false,
});
const string = (maxLength = 100) => ({
  type: "string",
  minLength: 1,
  maxLength,
});
const actorProperties = {
  id: string(),
  role: string(40),
  roles: {
    type: "array",
    minItems: 1,
    maxItems: 20,
    uniqueItems: true,
    items: string(40),
  },
  organization_id: { type: ["string", "null"], maxLength: 100 },
  team_ids: {
    type: "array",
    maxItems: 100,
    uniqueItems: true,
    items: string(),
  },
  name: string(),
  authorization_revision: { type: "integer", minimum: 1 },
};
const actorSchema = objectSchema(actorProperties);
const requestId = { type: "string", pattern: "^[0-9a-f]{32}$" };
const workflowCheck = compile(
  objectSchema({
    protocol_version: { type: "string", const: "1.0" },
    request_id: requestId,
    context: objectSchema({ actor: actorSchema }),
    input: { type: "object" },
  }),
);
const invokeActor = {
  ...actorSchema,
  properties: {
    ...actorProperties,
    role: {
      ...string(40),
      enum: [
        "admin",
        "member",
        "researcher",
        "contributor",
        "curator",
        "reviewer",
        "system_admin",
      ],
    },
    roles: {
      ...actorProperties.roles,
      maxItems: 7,
      items: {
        ...string(40),
        enum: [
          "admin",
          "member",
          "researcher",
          "contributor",
          "curator",
          "reviewer",
          "system_admin",
        ],
      },
    },
  },
};
const invokeCheck = compile(
  objectSchema({
    protocol_version: { type: "string", const: "1.1" },
    request_id: requestId,
    capability_id: { type: "string", pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{2,120}$" },
    capability_version: string(40),
    input: { type: "object" },
    context: objectSchema({ workspace_id: string(), actor: invokeActor }),
  }),
);
async function bodyOf(request, limit = 65536) {
  const chunks = [];
  let size = 0;
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += chunk.length;
    requireThat(size <= limit, "request_size", "请求超过接口大小限制。", 413);
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
  } catch {
    throw fail("invalid_json", "请求不是有效 JSON。");
  }
}
function send(response, status, data) {
  const body = JSON.stringify(data);
  response.writeHead(status, {
    "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
  });
  response.end(body);
}
export function createModuleServer({
  manifest,
  registration,
  token,
  handlers,
  workflowHandlers = {},
  graph = null,
  observationClient = null,
}) {
  // Snapshot the same dispatch tables that acceptance checks. Caller mutation
  // after startup must not silently remove a previously confirmed interface.
  manifest = structuredClone(manifest);
  registration = structuredClone(registration);
  handlers = Object.freeze({ ...handlers });
  workflowHandlers = Object.freeze({ ...workflowHandlers });
  const atoms = graph
    ? createAtomBindings({ graph, manifest, handlers })
    : null;
  requireThat(
    typeof token === "string" && token.length >= 24,
    "service_config",
    "请配置至少 24 字符的私有服务凭据。",
    500,
  );
  const expected = Buffer.from(`Bearer ${token}`);
  const capabilities = new Map(
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => [
      cap.id,
      {
        cap,
        input: compile(cap.input_schema),
        output: compile(cap.output_schema),
      },
    ]),
  );
  const registrar = createRegistration({
    manifest,
    registration,
    handlers,
    workflowHandlers,
    compile,
    graph: atoms?.document,
    runtimeProbe: observationClient?.probe,
  });
  return http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
    let observation;
    try {
      const received = Buffer.from(request.headers.authorization || "");
      requireThat(
        received.length === expected.length &&
          timingSafeEqual(received, expected),
        "service_auth",
        "服务连接认证失败。",
        401,
      );
      if (request.method === "GET" && request.url === "/platform/v1/health")
        return send(response, 200, { status: "ok" });
      if (request.method === "GET" && request.url === "/platform/v1/manifest")
        return send(response, 200, manifest);
      if (
        atoms &&
        request.method === "GET" &&
        request.url === "/platform/v1/module-graph"
      )
        return send(response, 200, atoms.document);
      if (
        registration.flow_exports &&
        request.method === "GET" &&
        request.url === "/platform/v1/flow-exports"
      )
        return send(response, 200, registrar.document.flow_exports);
      if (
        request.method === "GET" &&
        request.url === "/platform/v1/registration"
      )
        return send(response, 200, registrar.document);
      if (request.method === "POST" && request.url === "/platform/v1/handshake")
        return send(
          response,
          200,
          await registrar.confirm(await bodyOf(request, 1048576)),
        );
      const operation = request.url?.startsWith("/platform/v1/workflow/")
        ? request.url.slice("/platform/v1/workflow/".length)
        : null;
      requireThat(
        request.method === "POST" &&
          (operation || request.url === "/platform/v1/invoke"),
        "not_found",
        "接口不存在。",
        404,
      );
      const body = await bodyOf(request);
      requireThat(
        (operation ? workflowCheck : invokeCheck)(body) &&
          body.context.actor.roles.includes(body.context.actor.role),
        "input_contract",
        "请求包络不符合契约。",
      );
      // Access tickets are ephemeral arguments only: never included in body fingerprints, responses or persistence.
      const ticket = request.headers["x-platform-access"];
      if (registration.permissions.mode === "platform_access") {
        requireThat(
          typeof ticket === "string" &&
            ticket.length > 0 &&
            ticket.length <= 12288,
          "access_denied",
          "缺少有效的中央授权票据。",
          403,
        );
      }
      if (operation) {
        requireThat(
          Object.hasOwn(workflowHandlers, operation),
          "workflow_unsupported",
          "此服务未注册该对象接口。",
          501,
        );
        const data = await workflowHandlers[operation]({
          body,
          operation,
          ticket,
        });
        requireThat(
          Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(data)) <=
            (operation === "objects.list" ? 131072 : 1048576),
          "response_size",
          "模块响应超出契约上限。",
          500,
        );
        return send(response, 200, {
          protocol_version: "1.0",
          request_id: body.request_id,
          data,
        });
      }
      const spec = capabilities.get(body.capability_id);
      requireThat(
        spec &&
          spec.cap.version === body.capability_version &&
          spec.input(body.input),
        "input_contract",
        "能力、版本或参数不符合契约。",
      );
      if (registration.permissions.mode === "capability_roles") {
        requireThat(
          spec.cap.roles.some((role) =>
            body.context.actor.roles.includes(role),
          ),
          "access_denied",
          "当前身份无权使用此功能。",
          403,
        );
      }
      if (atoms && observationClient) {
        observation = observationClient.session({
          ticket: request.headers["x-duoflow-observation"],
          requestId: body.request_id,
          atomId: atoms.forCapability(body.capability_id).id,
        });
        observation.mark("started");
      }
      const output = await handlers[body.capability_id]({
        body,
        ticket,
        observe: observation,
      });
      requireThat(
        typeof output.summary === "string" &&
          output.summary.length <= 4000 &&
          spec.output(output.data) &&
          Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(output)) <= 1048576,
        "output_contract",
        "模块输出不符合契约。",
        500,
      );
      observation?.mark("succeeded");
      return send(response, 200, {
        protocol_version: "1.1",
        request_id: body.request_id,
        capability_id: body.capability_id,
        capability_version: body.capability_version,
        summary: output.summary,
        data: output.data,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      observation?.mark("failed");
      const safe =
        Number.isInteger(error.status) &&
        error.status >= 400 &&
        error.status < 500;
      send(response, Number.isInteger(error.status) ? error.status : 500, {
        error: {
          code: error.code || "handler_failed",
          message: safe ? error.message : "服务暂时无法完成此操作。",
        },
      });
    }
  });
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/store.mjs

```javascript
/** SQLite unit of work: one connection, serial async operations, atomic business + receipt commit. */
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import {
  createHash,
  randomBytes,
  createHmac,
  timingSafeEqual,
} from "node:crypto";
import { canonical, fail, requireThat } from "./runtime.mjs";

export class Store {
  constructor(filename) {
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(filename);
    this.db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA busy_timeout=4000;
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kit_receipts(request_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL, object_id TEXT NOT NULL, response TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kit_metadata(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);`);
    this.db
      .prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO kit_metadata(key,value) VALUES (?,?)")
      .run("cursor-key", randomBytes(32).toString("hex"));
    this.cursorKey = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT value FROM kit_metadata WHERE key=?")
      .get("cursor-key").value;
    this.queue = Promise.resolve();
  }
  transaction(callback) {
    const work = this.queue.then(async () => {
      this.db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
      try {
        const result = await callback(this.db);
        this.db.exec("COMMIT");
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
        throw error;
      }
    });
    this.queue = work.catch(() => {});
    return work;
  }
  fingerprint(request) {
    return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(request)).digest("hex");
  }
  receipt(request) {
    const saved = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM kit_receipts WHERE request_id=?")
      .get(request.request_id);
    if (saved)
      requireThat(
        saved.fingerprint === this.fingerprint(request),
        "idempotency_conflict",
        "同一请求编号的内容已经变化。",
        409,
      );
    return saved
      ? { objectId: saved.object_id, response: JSON.parse(saved.response) }
      : null;
  }
  saveReceipt(request, objectId, response) {
    this.db
      .prepare("INSERT INTO kit_receipts VALUES (?,?,?,?)")
      .run(
        request.request_id,
        this.fingerprint(request),
        objectId,
        JSON.stringify(response),
      );
  }
  cursor(binding, offset) {
    const payload = Buffer.from(canonical({ binding, offset })).toString(
      "base64url",
    );
    return `${payload}.${createHmac("sha256", this.cursorKey).update(payload).digest("base64url")}`;
  }
  offset(cursor, binding) {
    if (!cursor) return 0;
    try {
      const [payload, signature, extra] = cursor.split(".");
      const expected = Buffer.from(
        createHmac("sha256", this.cursorKey)
          .update(payload)
          .digest("base64url"),
      );
      const actual = Buffer.from(signature || "");
      if (
        extra ||
        expected.length !== actual.length ||
        !timingSafeEqual(actual, expected)
      )
        throw new Error();
      const decoded = JSON.parse(
        Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString("utf8"),
      );
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(decoded.offset) || decoded.offset < 0)
        throw new Error();
      requireThat(
        canonical(decoded.binding) === canonical(binding),
        "revision_conflict",
        "目录或授权已变化，请从第一页重新读取。",
        409,
      );
      return decoded.offset;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === "revision_conflict") throw error;
      throw fail("cursor_invalid", "分页游标无效。");
    }
  }
  async close() {
    await this.queue;
    this.db.close();
  }
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/README.md

````markdown
# Python 工具 SDK 1.0.0

`graph_testing.py` 提供 `assert_transition(graph, machine_id, before, event, after)` 与 `assert_atom_result(graph, atom_id, inputs, output, validate)`。测试先调用真实业务代码，再传入实际读到的对象或结果。后端原子另传 `manifest=实际清单`，函数按能力 ID 和版本读取 Schema。`validate(schema, value)` 由项目提供，校验失败时抛出异常。前者核对同一对象的状态变化是否有声明，后者核对原子输入输出；权限、修订和实际数据效果仍需独立断言。

已有 Python HTTP 服务另使用同目录 `registration.py` 主动核对注册握手，以及 `remote_access.py` 请求中央授权。注册确认器不替代 HTTP 认证或实际调用适配器，使用方法见同批 `docs/模块框架说明.md`。原生工具不进行远程握手。

`module_kit.py` 为标准库单文件，脚手架复制为生成包的 `backend/_module_kit.py`。安装时整个 backend 目录一起复制。没有 PyPI 安装、全局路径修改或对本仓库工作目录的依赖。

稳定入口：

```python
run_tool(ctx, permission="your.module.use", payload=payload, handler=business_function)
require_organization_access(ctx, "your.module.use")
```

`ctx` 必须来自平台，提供认证后的 `actor` 与真实 `access.check(actor, permission, facts)`。本 SDK 只读取认证身份中的 `organization_id`，采用 `allowed is True` 默认拒绝，不包含 Casbin、角色回退或本地授权缓存。业务函数接收 JSON 对象，返回 `{"summary": "说明", "data": {...}}`；输入上限 100000 UTF-8 字节，结果上限 256000 字节。`ValueError` 映射为通用输入错误，其余业务异常交给平台安全错误处理。`KitError` 提供 code、message、status，由原生处理器转换成平台错误。

该入口仅用于没有持久对象的机构内纯函数工具。正式对象、仓储事实、事务、职责状态、修订、幂等和副作用不由它实现；不要用操作者机构替代已有对象的机构。平台仍验证能力输入输出 Schema、访问快照和宿主请求安全。复制后 SDK 是模块发布的一部分；框架升级不会自动改写已交付副本，升级要对照契约、替换 SDK 并测试。
````

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/atoms.py

```python
"""Bind graph atoms to the real dispatch table without calling business code.

Run the kit's structural/host checks before deployment. This startup check has a
different purpose: a handshake may acknowledge only callable local bindings.
"""

from copy import deepcopy
from types import MappingProxyType


class AtomBindings:
    def __init__(self, graph, manifest, handlers):
        """Pin one definition per actual capability and reject missing callables."""
        module = manifest.get("module", manifest)
        if graph.get("graph_version") != "1.0" or (graph["module_id"], graph["module_version"]) != (
            module["id"],
            module["version"],
        ):
            raise ValueError("Atom graph identity differs from the running module")
        capabilities = {item["id"]: item for item in manifest["capabilities"]}
        backends = [atom for atom in graph["atoms"] if atom["kind"] == "backend"]
        bound = {atom["capability"]["id"]: atom for atom in backends}
        if (
            len(bound) != len(backends)
            or set(bound) != set(capabilities)
            or set(handlers) != set(capabilities)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Every capability requires one actual atomic binding")
        for identifier, atom in bound.items():
            if (
                not callable(handlers[identifier])
                or atom["capability"]["version"] != capabilities[identifier]["version"]
            ):
                raise ValueError("Atom handler or version is invalid")
        self._graph = deepcopy(graph)
        self._by_capability = deepcopy(bound)
        self.handlers = MappingProxyType(dict(handlers))

    @property
    def document(self):
        """Return a copy so callers cannot mutate accepted declarations."""
        return deepcopy(self._graph)

    def for_capability(self, identifier):
        """Resolve observation metadata; this lookup grants no execution rights."""
        return deepcopy(self._by_capability[identifier])
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/graph_testing.py

```python
"""Assertions over observed results. These helpers never execute business transitions."""


def assert_transition(graph, machine_id, before, event, after):
    """Compare independent before/after projections with a declared state edge.

    Call the real module handler between the two reads. Authorization, data
    effects and revision rules still require separate business assertions.
    """
    machines = [item for item in graph["state_machines"] if item["id"] == machine_id]
    if len(machines) != 1:
        raise AssertionError("Expected one declared state machine: " + machine_id)
    if before.get("object_ref") != after.get("object_ref"):
        raise AssertionError("Before and after refer to different business objects")
    edge = (before.get("state"), event, after.get("state"))
    if not any(
        (item["from"], item["event"], item["to"]) == edge for item in machines[0]["transitions"]
    ):
        raise AssertionError(f"Observed transition is absent from the graph: {edge!r}")


def assert_atom_result(graph, atom_id, inputs, output, validate, *, manifest=None):
    """Use the project's Schema validator on results returned by the real handler."""
    atoms = [item for item in graph["atoms"] if item["id"] == atom_id]
    if len(atoms) != 1:
        raise AssertionError("Expected one declared atom: " + atom_id)
    contract = atoms[0]
    if contract["kind"] == "backend":
        capabilities = (manifest or {}).get("capabilities", [])
        matching = [
            item
            for item in capabilities
            if item["id"] == contract["capability"]["id"]
            and item["version"] == contract["capability"]["version"]
        ]
        if len(matching) != 1:
            raise AssertionError("Backend atom requires its actual manifest capability")
        contract = matching[0]
    validate(contract["input_schema"], inputs)
    validate(contract["output_schema"], output)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/module_kit.py

```python
"""Portable module-kit v1 helpers for native Python tools (standard library only).

This file is vendored as backend/_module_kit.py. It is not an authorization
engine: the injected platform access service makes every permission decision.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json


SDK_VERSION = "1.0.0"
MAX_INPUT_BYTES = 100_000
MAX_RESULT_BYTES = 256_000


class KitError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, status: int = 400):
        self.code, self.message, self.status = code, message, status
        super().__init__(message)


def _json_size(value, limit: int, *, output: bool = False):
    try:
        encoded = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8")
    except (TypeError, ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
        raise KitError(
            "tool_result_invalid" if output else "tool_input_invalid",
            "工具结果不是有效 JSON。" if output else "输入不是有效 JSON。",
            500 if output else 422,
        ) from None
    if len(encoded) > limit:
        raise KitError(
            "tool_result_too_large" if output else "tool_input_too_large",
            "工具结果超过大小限制。" if output else "输入超过大小限制。",
            500 if output else 413,
        )


def require_organization_access(ctx, permission: str) -> dict:
    """Authorize the caller's organization, never a scope supplied in payload.

    Only appropriate for an actor-scoped tool with no stored business object.
    Object tools must instead derive facts from their own trusted repository.
    """
    actor = getattr(ctx, "actor", None)
    if (
        not isinstance(actor, dict)
        or not isinstance(actor.get("id"), str)
        or not actor["id"].strip()
        or actor.get("enabled") is False
    ):
        raise KitError("tool_identity_required", "需要有效的平台身份。", 403)
    organization_id = actor.get("organization_id")
    if (
        not isinstance(organization_id, str)
        or not organization_id.strip()
        or organization_id == "*"
    ):
        raise KitError("tool_organization_required", "请先由平台管理员分配所属机构。", 403)
    access = getattr(ctx, "access", None)
    if not callable(getattr(access, "check", None)):
        raise KitError("tool_access_unavailable", "平台权限服务不可用。", 503)
    facts = {"organization_id": organization_id}
    try:
        decision = access.check(actor, permission, facts)
    except Exception:
        # Do not expose provider errors or attempt a legacy-role fallback.
        raise KitError("tool_access_unavailable", "平台权限检查失败，请稍后重试。", 503) from None
    if not isinstance(decision, dict) or decision.get("allowed") is not True:
        raise KitError("tool_forbidden", "未获得本机构的工具使用授权。", 403)
    return facts


def run_tool(ctx, *, permission: str, payload: dict, handler) -> dict:
    """Check current scope and call a pure function returning summary + data.

    Platform input/output Schema validation, idempotency, CSRF, lifecycle and
    invocation history remain platform responsibilities. This wrapper neither
    persists records nor commits the injected transaction.
    """
    require_organization_access(ctx, permission)
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise KitError("tool_input_invalid", "输入必须是 JSON 对象。", 422)
    _json_size(payload, MAX_INPUT_BYTES)
    try:
        result = handler(payload)
    except ValueError:
        raise KitError(
            "tool_input_invalid", "输入不符合工具要求，请检查字段与内容。", 422
        ) from None
    if (
        not isinstance(result, dict)
        or set(result) != {"summary", "data"}
        or not isinstance(result["summary"], str)
        or not 1 <= len(result["summary"]) <= 4000
        or not isinstance(result["data"], dict)
    ):
        raise KitError("tool_result_invalid", "工具结果不符合 summary/data 契约。", 500)
    _json_size(result, MAX_RESULT_BYTES, output=True)
    return result
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/observation.py

```python
"""Server-only observation queue; delivery cannot change business outcomes."""

from copy import deepcopy
import json
from queue import Queue, Full
import re
from threading import Thread
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from urllib.request import Request, build_opener, HTTPRedirectHandler


class _NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return None


class ObservationClient:
    def __init__(self, callback_url, graph, *, timeout=1, send=None):
        """Use administrator configuration for the destination, never request input."""
        url = urlsplit(callback_url)
        if (
            url.scheme not in {"http", "https"}
            or not url.netloc
            or url.username
            or url.password
            or url.query
            or url.fragment
            or url.path != "/api/v1/runtime-observations/events"
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid configured observation endpoint")
        if not 0 < timeout <= 2:
            raise ValueError("Observation timeout must be at most two seconds")
        self.url, self.graph, self.timeout = callback_url, deepcopy(graph), timeout
        self.send = send or self._send
        self.queue = Queue(maxsize=64)
        self.worker = Thread(target=self._deliver, daemon=True, name="duoflow-observations")
        self.worker.start()

    def _send(self, ticket, batch):
        request = Request(
            self.url,
            data=json.dumps(batch).encode(),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "X-DuoFlow-Observation": ticket},
            method="POST",
        )
        with build_opener(_NoRedirect).open(request, timeout=self.timeout) as response:
            return response.status == 200

    def _deliver(self):
        """Bound retries; do not serialize credentials or report transport errors as business errors."""
        while True:
            ticket, batch, lost = self.queue.get()
            try:
                for _ in range(2):
                    try:
                        if self.send(ticket, batch):
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                else:
                    lost()
            finally:
                self.queue.task_done()

    def session(self, *, ticket, request_id, atom_id):
        """Create a call-local reporter; absent tickets produce no network traffic."""
        return ObservationSession(self, ticket, request_id, atom_id)

    def probe(self, challenge):
        """Actively confirm the platform callback using its one-time challenge."""
        if (
            not isinstance(challenge, dict)
            or set(challenge) != {"ticket", "nonce"}
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", str(challenge["ticket"]))
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", str(challenge["nonce"]))
        ):
            return False
        url = self.url.rsplit("/", 1)[0] + "/probe"
        body = {"runtime_version": "1.0", "nonce": challenge["nonce"]}
        try:
            request = Request(
                url,
                data=json.dumps(body).encode(),
                headers={
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "X-DuoFlow-Observation": challenge["ticket"],
                },
                method="POST",
            )
            with build_opener(_NoRedirect).open(request, timeout=self.timeout) as response:
                raw = response.read(1025)
                if len(raw) > 1024:
                    return False
                result = json.loads(raw)
                return response.status == 200 and result == {
                    "accepted": True,
                    "nonce": challenge["nonce"],
                }
        except Exception:
            return False

    def flush(self):
        """Wait for the bounded queue during tests or controlled shutdown."""
        self.queue.join()


class ObservationSession:
    def __init__(self, client, ticket, request_id, atom_id):
        self.client, self.ticket, self.request_id, self.atom_id = (
            client,
            ticket,
            request_id,
            atom_id,
        )
        self.sequence, self.dropped = 0, 0

    def _lost(self):
        self.dropped = min(256, self.dropped + 1)

    def _emit(self, phase, state=None):
        if (
            not isinstance(self.ticket, str)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", self.ticket)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", self.request_id)
        ):
            return
        if self.sequence >= 256:
            self._lost()
            return
        self.sequence += 1
        event = {
            "sequence": self.sequence,
            "atom_id": self.atom_id,
            "call_id": self.request_id,
            "parent_call_id": None,
            "phase": phase,
        }
        if state is not None:
            event["state"] = deepcopy(state)
        batch = {
            "runtime_version": "1.0",
            "request_id": self.request_id,
            "graph_version": self.client.graph["version"],
            "dropped": self.dropped,
            "events": [event],
        }
        try:
            self.client.queue.put_nowait((self.ticket, batch, self._lost))
        except Full:
            self._lost()

    def mark(self, phase):
        """Report the actual invocation boundary, without affecting dispatch."""
        if phase in {"started", "succeeded", "failed"}:
            self._emit(phase)

    def state(self, machine_id, projection):
        """Report a committed or freshly read projection, omitting business data."""
        if (
            not isinstance(projection, dict)
            or not projection.get("object_ref")
            or type(projection.get("revision")) is not int
            or not isinstance(projection.get("state"), str)
        ):
            return
        self._emit(
            "state",
            {
                "machine_id": machine_id,
                "value": projection["state"],
                "revision": projection["revision"],
                "object_ref": projection["object_ref"],
            },
        )
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/registration.py

```python
"""Portable bilateral registration v1 for an existing Python HTTP adapter.

The adapter authenticates the connection before calling confirm. Handlers must
be the actual dispatch map, not a second list of claimed function names. This
module performs no business calls, database access or user authorization grants.
"""

from copy import deepcopy
import re
from types import MappingProxyType

from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator


class RegistrationError(ValueError):
    """Map to HTTP 422 with fixed wording, never echo a received contract."""


class Registration:
    def __init__(
        self,
        manifest,
        document,
        handlers,
        workflow_handlers=None,
        *,
        graph=None,
        runtime_probe=None
    ):
        """Validate startup bindings and pin complete local declarations."""
        workflow_handlers = workflow_handlers or {}
        self._need(
            document.get("registration_version") in {"1.0", "1.1"}
            and manifest["protocol_version"] in ("1.0", "1.1", "1.2")
        )
        self._need(
            (document["module_id"], document["module_version"])
            == (manifest["module"]["id"], manifest["module"]["version"])
        )
        self.ids = sorted(cap["id"] for cap in manifest["capabilities"])
        self.modern = document["registration_version"] == "1.1"
        self.runtime_probe = runtime_probe
        if self.modern:
            from .atoms import AtomBindings

            self._need(graph == document["module_graph"] and callable(runtime_probe))
            AtomBindings(graph, manifest, handlers)
        self._need(sorted(handlers) == self.ids and all(callable(fn) for fn in handlers.values()))
        self._need(
            sorted(workflow_handlers) == document["workflow_operations"]
            and all(callable(fn) for fn in workflow_handlers.values())
        )
        for cap in manifest["capabilities"]:
            for field in ("input_schema", "output_schema"):
                Draft202012Validator.check_schema(cap[field])
        self._manifest, self._document = deepcopy(manifest), deepcopy(document)
        self.handlers = MappingProxyType(dict(handlers))
        self.workflow_handlers = MappingProxyType(dict(workflow_handlers))

    @property
    def manifest(self):
        """Expose a copy, keeping the contract checked at startup immutable."""
        return deepcopy(self._manifest)

    @property
    def document(self):
        """Return the local public declaration without exposing mutable storage."""
        return deepcopy(self._document)

    @staticmethod
    def _need(condition):
        if not condition:
            raise RegistrationError("注册确认未通过，请检查协议、声明与接口绑定。")

    def confirm(self, offer):
        """Actively compare the platform offer, then acknowledge its unique nonce."""
        self._need(
            isinstance(offer, dict)
            and set(offer)
            == (
                {
                    "registration_version",
                    "nonce",
                    "contract_digest",
                    "platform_features",
                    "manifest",
                    "registration",
                }
                | ({"runtime_probe"} if self.modern else set())
            )
        )
        self._need(offer["registration_version"] == self.document["registration_version"])
        for key, length in (("nonce", 32), ("contract_digest", 64)):
            self._need(
                isinstance(offer[key], str) and re.fullmatch("[0-9a-f]{%d}" % length, offer[key])
            )
        required = {
            "invoke-1.1" if self.manifest["protocol_version"] == "1.2" else "invoke-1.0",
            "shared-hosts-1.0",
            "registration-1.0",
        }
        if self.document["permissions"]["mode"] == "platform_access":
            required.add("access-1.0")
        if self.modern:
            required.update(
                {
                    "registration-1.1",
                    "module-graph-1.0",
                    "flow-exports-1.0",
                    "runtime-observation-1.0",
                }
            )
        self._need(
            isinstance(offer["platform_features"], list)
            and all(isinstance(value, str) for value in offer["platform_features"])
        )
        self._need(required.issubset(offer["platform_features"]))
        self._need(offer["manifest"] == self.manifest and offer["registration"] == self.document)
        if self.modern:
            self._need(self.runtime_probe(offer["runtime_probe"]) is True)
        return {
            "registration_version": self.document["registration_version"],
            "nonce": offer["nonce"],
            "contract_digest": offer["contract_digest"],
            "accepted": True,
            "checks": {
                "platform_contract": True,
                "local_manifest": True,
                "local_registration": True,
                "handlers": True,
                "schemas": True,
                **({"atomic_bindings": True, "runtime_callback": True} if self.modern else {}),
            },
            "capability_ids": self.ids,
            "workflow_operations": self.document["workflow_operations"],
        }
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/remote_access.py

```python
"""Portable access v1 client for trusted Python remote services (Python 3.11+).

Validate the incoming service Bearer first, read facts from your repository,
then call ``decide`` with the original request ID, operation and access ticket.
This client neither authenticates browser input nor replaces a state machine,
business revision checks or idempotency. Tickets must not be saved or logged.
Only httpx and the standard library are required; no platform imports occur.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hmac
import json
import math
import re
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx


_ENDPOINT = "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions"
_MAX_REQUEST = 16 * 1024
_MAX_TICKET = 12 * 1024
_MAX_RESPONSE = 4096
_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}")
_FACT_KEY = re.compile(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}")
# Return public reason codes only, never arbitrary text supplied by a server.
# New central deny reasons remain denied as access_decision_invalid until this
# portable client's allowlist is updated; they cannot expand authorization.
_DENIAL_REASONS = frozenset(
    {
        "permission_unknown",
        "identity_inactive",
        "module_disabled",
        "policy_revision_changed",
        "required_fact_missing",
        "separation_of_duties",
        "actor_relation_mismatch",
        "fact_condition_failed",
        "grant_scope_mismatch",
        "command_unmapped",
        "access_ticket_invalid",
        "access_ticket_expired",
        "access_ticket_request_mismatch",
        "access_ticket_operation_mismatch",
        "access_decision_contract",
        "access_authorization_changed",
        "access_bridge_binding",
        "access_bridge_unavailable",
        "access_ticket_permission_mismatch",
        "actor_disabled",
        "authorization_changed",
        "capability_missing",
        "forbidden",
    }
)


def _deny(reason):
    return {"allowed": False, "reason": reason, "policy_revision": 0}


def service_bearer_matches(authorization: str | None, expected_token: str) -> bool:
    """Compare the configured service secret in constant time; never default open."""
    if not isinstance(authorization, str) or not isinstance(expected_token, str):
        return False
    if not expected_token or any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) > 126 for c in expected_token):
        return False
    scheme, separator, token = authorization.partition(" ")
    if scheme.lower() != "bearer" or separator != " " or not token:
        return False
    if any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) > 126 for c in token):
        return False
    return hmac.compare_digest(token.encode("ascii"), expected_token.encode("ascii"))


def _valid_request(request_id, operation, permission, facts):
    if not all(
        isinstance(v, str) and _IDENTIFIER.fullmatch(v) for v in (request_id, operation, permission)
    ):
        return False
    if not isinstance(facts, dict) or len(facts) > 32:
        return False
    for key, value in facts.items():
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not _FACT_KEY.fullmatch(key):
            return False
        if isinstance(value, str):
            if not 1 <= len(value) <= 160:
                return False
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            if not 1 <= len(value) <= 20 or not all(
                isinstance(v, str) and 1 <= len(v) <= 160 for v in value
            ):
                return False
            if len(set(value)) != len(value):
                return False
        else:
            return False
    return True


def _unique_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError("Duplicate JSON field")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _invalid_constant(_value):
    raise ValueError("Non-finite JSON number")


class RemoteAccessClient:
    """Fixed deployment callback, bounded I/O and fail-closed central decisions.

    ``transport`` is the standard httpx test/transport seam. Each call closes its
    own HTTP client and never stores request facts or tickets on this instance.
    Caller cancellation propagates; operational failures return a safe denial.
    """

    def __init__(self, callback_url: str, *, timeout_seconds: float = 4.0, transport=None):
        try:
            if not isinstance(callback_url, str) or any(
                ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) == 127 for c in callback_url
            ):
                raise ValueError
            endpoint = urlsplit(callback_url)
            if (
                endpoint.scheme not in {"http", "https"}
                or not endpoint.hostname
                or endpoint.username is not None
                or endpoint.password is not None
                or endpoint.path != _ENDPOINT
                or "?" in callback_url
                or "#" in callback_url
                or (endpoint.port is not None and not 1 <= endpoint.port <= 65535)
            ):
                raise ValueError
            # Validate the actual HTTP library's URL parser as well.
            parsed = httpx.URL(callback_url)
            if parsed.raw_path != _ENDPOINT.encode("ascii") or not parsed.host:
                raise ValueError
        except (TypeError, ValueError, httpx.InvalidURL):
            raise ValueError("Invalid configured access-decision endpoint") from None
        if (
            type(timeout_seconds) not in (int, float)
            or not math.isfinite(timeout_seconds)
            or not 0 < timeout_seconds <= 10
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Access decision timeout must be greater than zero and at most 10 seconds"
            )
        self._callback_url = parsed
        self._timeout_seconds = float(timeout_seconds)
        self._transport = transport

    async def decide(self, *, ticket, request_id, operation, permission, facts) -> dict:
        if (
            not isinstance(ticket, str)
            or not 0 < len(ticket) <= _MAX_TICKET
            or any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) > 126 for c in ticket)
        ):
            return _deny("access_ticket_invalid")
        if not _valid_request(request_id, operation, permission, facts):
            return _deny("access_decision_contract")
        try:
            body = json.dumps(
                {
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "operation": operation,
                    "permission": permission,
                    "facts": facts,
                },
                ensure_ascii=False,
                allow_nan=False,
                separators=(",", ":"),
            ).encode("utf-8")
        except (TypeError, ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
            return _deny("access_decision_contract")
        if len(body) > _MAX_REQUEST:
            return _deny("access_decision_size")
        try:
            # A total deadline also bounds custom transports and slow trickles.
            async with asyncio.timeout(self._timeout_seconds):
                async with httpx.AsyncClient(
                    transport=self._transport,
                    trust_env=False,
                    follow_redirects=False,
                    timeout=self._timeout_seconds,
                ) as client:
                    async with client.stream(
                        "POST",
                        self._callback_url,
                        content=body,
                        headers={
                            "Content-Type": "application/json",
                            "Accept-Encoding": "identity",
                            "X-Platform-Access": ticket,
                        },
                    ) as response:
                        if response.status_code != 200:
                            return _deny("access_decision_unavailable")
                        # Reject compressed bodies to avoid decompression expansion.
                        if (
                            response.headers.get("content-encoding", "identity").lower()
                            != "identity"
                        ):
                            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
                        length = response.headers.get("content-length")
                        if length is not None and (
                            not length.isdigit() or int(length) > _MAX_RESPONSE
                        ):
                            return _deny("access_decision_response_size")
                        received = bytearray()
                        async for chunk in response.aiter_bytes():
                            if len(received) + len(chunk) > _MAX_RESPONSE:
                                return _deny("access_decision_response_size")
                            received.extend(chunk)
            result = json.loads(
                received.decode("utf-8"),
                object_pairs_hook=_unique_object,
                parse_constant=_invalid_constant,
            )
        except (ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, TimeoutError):
            return _deny("access_decision_unavailable")
        if (
            not isinstance(result, dict)
            or set(result) != {"allowed", "reason", "policy_revision"}
            or type(result["allowed"]) is not bool
            or not isinstance(result["reason"], str)
            or type(result["policy_revision"]) is not int
            or result["policy_revision"] < 0
        ):
            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        if result["allowed"]:
            if result["reason"] != "allowed" or result["policy_revision"] < 1:
                return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        elif result["reason"] not in _DENIAL_REASONS:
            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        return {key: result[key] for key in ("allowed", "reason", "policy_revision")}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_graph_testing.py

```python
"""Contract negatives for observation-based assertions, separate from business tests."""

from pathlib import Path
from runpy import run_path

import pytest
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

HELPERS = run_path(str(Path(__file__).parents[1] / "graph_testing.py"))


def test_transition_rejects_wrong_object_and_undeclared_result():
    graph = {
        "state_machines": [
            {
                "id": "record",
                "transitions": [{"from": "draft", "event": "submit", "to": "submitted"}],
            }
        ]
    }
    before = {"object_ref": {"object_id": "one"}, "state": "draft"}
    after = {**before, "state": "submitted"}
    check = HELPERS["assert_transition"]
    check(graph, "record", before, "submit", after)
    for value in ({**after, "state": "approved"}, {**after, "object_ref": {"object_id": "two"}}):
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
            check(graph, "record", before, "submit", value)


def test_atom_result_checks_real_values_in_both_directions():
    graph = {
        "atoms": [
            {
                "id": "read",
                "kind": "frontend",
                "input_schema": {"type": "string"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "integer"},
            }
        ]
    }
    validate = lambda schema, value: Draft202012Validator(schema).validate(value)
    check = HELPERS["assert_atom_result"]
    check(graph, "read", "key", 2, validate)
    from jsonschema import ValidationError

    for inputs, output in ((2, 2), ("key", "wrong")):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            check(graph, "read", inputs, output, validate)


def test_backend_atom_resolves_the_matching_manifest_version():
    graph = {
        "atoms": [{"id": "read", "kind": "backend", "capability": {"id": "read", "version": "1"}}]
    }
    manifest = {
        "capabilities": [
            {
                "id": "read",
                "version": "1",
                "input_schema": {"type": "string"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "integer"},
            }
        ]
    }
    check = HELPERS["assert_atom_result"]
    validate = lambda schema, value: Draft202012Validator(schema).validate(value)
    check(graph, "read", "key", 2, validate, manifest=manifest)
    manifest["capabilities"][0]["version"] = "2"
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
        check(graph, "read", "key", 2, validate, manifest=manifest)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_remote_access.py

```python
"""Portable client boundaries; no platform installation or application imports."""

import asyncio
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest


SOURCE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "remote_access.py"
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("kit_portable_remote_access", SOURCE)
sdk = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(sdk)
RemoteAccessClient = sdk.RemoteAccessClient
service_bearer_matches = sdk.service_bearer_matches
ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1/api/v1/access/remote-decisions"
SUCCESS = {"allowed": True, "reason": "allowed", "policy_revision": 2}
REQUEST = dict(
    ticket="synthetic-ticket",
    request_id="req:one",
    operation="objects.read",
    permission="sample.item.read",
    facts={"project_id": "P", "owner_id": "alice"},
)


def run(client, **changes):
    return asyncio.run(client.decide(**{**REQUEST, **changes}))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "url",
    [
        "https://example.test/wrong",
        "ftp://example.test/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "http:///api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "https://user:secret@example.test/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "https://@example.test/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        ENDPOINT + "?q=1",
        ENDPOINT + "?",
        ENDPOINT + "#fragment",
        ENDPOINT + "#",
        ENDPOINT + "/",
        ENDPOINT + "\n",
        "http://127.0.0.1:0/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "http://127.0.0.1:99999/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "http://127.0.0.1/api/v1/access/%72emote-decisions",
        None,
    ],
)
def test_callback_rejects_nonfixed_or_credential_bearing_urls(url):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid configured"):
        RemoteAccessClient(url)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("timeout", [0, -1, 10.1, True, None, "4", float("nan"), float("inf")])
def test_timeout_is_finite_bounded_and_not_boolean(timeout):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timeout"):
        RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, timeout_seconds=timeout)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "header,secret,expected",
    [
        ("Bearer synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", True),
        ("bEaReR synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", True),
        (None, "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer ", "", False),
        ("Bearer wrong", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Basic synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer  synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer synthetic-secret\n", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer 合成", "合成", False),
        ("Bearer synthetic-secret", None, False),
    ],
)
def test_service_bearer_missing_or_malformed_never_defaults_open(header, secret, expected):
    assert service_bearer_matches(header, secret) is expected


def test_service_bearer_uses_constant_time_comparison(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    original = sdk.hmac.compare_digest
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        sdk.hmac, "compare_digest", lambda a, b: (calls.append((a, b)), original(a, b))[1]
    )
    assert service_bearer_matches("Bearer synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret")
    assert calls == [(b"synthetic-secret", b"synthetic-secret")]


def test_exact_body_ticket_header_and_environment_isolation(monkeypatch):
    observations = []
    client_options = []
    real_client = sdk.httpx.AsyncClient

    def construct(**kwargs):
        client_options.append(kwargs)
        return real_client(**kwargs)

    def callback(request):
        observations.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=SUCCESS)

    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk.httpx, "AsyncClient", construct)
    client = RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback))
    assert run(client) == SUCCESS
    request = observations[0]
    assert str(request.url) == ENDPOINT and request.method == "POST"
    assert request.headers["X-Platform-Access"] == REQUEST["ticket"]
    assert "authorization" not in request.headers
    assert json.loads(request.content) == {k: v for k, v in REQUEST.items() if k != "ticket"}
    assert REQUEST["ticket"].encode() not in request.content
    assert client_options[0]["trust_env"] is False
    assert client_options[0]["follow_redirects"] is False
    assert client_options[0]["timeout"] == 4.0
    assert not any("ticket" in name or "facts" in name for name in vars(client))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "changes,reason",
    [
        ({"ticket": None}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": ""}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": "x" * (12 * 1024 + 1)}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": "x\r\nInjected: value"}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": "非ASCII"}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"request_id": "bad/id"}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"operation": "invoke\n"}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"permission": "a" * 161}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"owner_id": None}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"owner_id": True}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"Owner": "alice"}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"teams": ["a", "a"]}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"teams": []}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"teams": [str(i) for i in range(21)]}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {f"fact_{i}": "value" for i in range(33)}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        (
            {"facts": {f"fact_{i}": [str(j) + "合" * 150 for j in range(20)] for i in range(3)}},
            "access_decision_size",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_invalid_input_is_denied_before_transport(changes, reason):
    def callback(_request):
        pytest.fail("Invalid input must not reach the callback")

    result = run(RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback)), **changes)
    assert result == {"allowed": False, "reason": reason, "policy_revision": 0}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "body",
    [
        {},
        [],
        {**SUCCESS, "actor": {"id": "private"}},
        {**SUCCESS, "allowed": 1},
        {**SUCCESS, "reason": "grant_scope_mismatch"},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": True},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": 0},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": -1},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": 1.5},
        {**SUCCESS, "reason": None},
        {"allowed": False, "reason": "private-provider-message", "policy_revision": 0},
        {"allowed": False, "reason": "allowed", "policy_revision": 1},
    ],
)
def test_invalid_response_fields_fail_closed(body):
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, json=body))
    )
    assert run(client) == {
        "allowed": False,
        "reason": "access_decision_invalid",
        "policy_revision": 0,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "raw",
    [
        b"not JSON private content",
        b"\xff",
        b'{"allowed":true,"reason":"allowed","policy_revision":NaN}',
        b'{"allowed":false,"allowed":true,"reason":"allowed","policy_revision":2}',
    ],
)
def test_bad_json_duplicate_keys_and_nonfinite_never_escape(raw):
    result = run(
        RemoteAccessClient(
            ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, content=raw))
        )
    )
    assert result == {"allowed": False, "reason": "access_decision_invalid", "policy_revision": 0}


def test_normal_denial_is_preserved():
    denial = {"allowed": False, "reason": "grant_scope_mismatch", "policy_revision": 3}
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, json=denial))
    )
    assert run(client) == denial


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [201, 302, 307, 401, 403, 429, 500])
def test_http_failure_or_redirect_never_follows_destination(status):
    calls = []

    def callback(request):
        calls.append(str(request.url))
        return httpx.Response(
            status,
            headers={"Location": "https://must-not-follow.invalid/secret"},
            content=b"private provider text",
        )

    result = run(RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback)))
    assert result == {
        "allowed": False,
        "reason": "access_decision_unavailable",
        "policy_revision": 0,
    }
    assert calls == [ENDPOINT]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "error", [httpx.ConnectError("private address"), httpx.ReadTimeout("private timeout")]
)
def test_network_failure_does_not_expose_exception(error):
    def callback(_request):
        raise error

    result = run(RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback)))
    assert result == {
        "allowed": False,
        "reason": "access_decision_unavailable",
        "policy_revision": 0,
    }


def test_total_deadline_also_applies_to_custom_transport():
    async def callback(_request):
        await asyncio.sleep(1)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=SUCCESS)

    result = run(
        RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, timeout_seconds=0.01, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback))
    )
    assert result["reason"] == "access_decision_unavailable" and result["allowed"] is False


class Chunks(httpx.AsyncByteStream):
    def __init__(self, chunks):
        self.chunks, self.read, self.closed = chunks, 0, False

    async def __aiter__(self):
        for chunk in self.chunks:
            self.read += 1
            yield chunk

    async def aclose(self):
        self.closed = True


def test_stream_stops_at_byte_limit_and_closes_response():
    chunks = Chunks([b" " * 2048, b" " * 2048, b" ", b"private remainder"])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, stream=chunks))
    )
    assert run(client)["reason"] == "access_decision_response_size"
    assert chunks.read == 3 and chunks.closed


def test_exact_response_limit_is_accepted():
    body = json.dumps(SUCCESS).encode()
    chunks = Chunks([body, b" " * (4096 - len(body))])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, stream=chunks))
    )
    assert run(client) == SUCCESS and chunks.closed


def test_announced_oversized_response_is_not_read():
    chunks = Chunks([b"private body"])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT,
        transport=httpx.MockTransport(
            lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, headers={"Content-Length": "4097"}, stream=chunks)
        ),
    )
    assert run(client)["reason"] == "access_decision_response_size"
    assert chunks.read == 0 and chunks.closed


def test_compressed_response_is_rejected_without_reading():
    chunks = Chunks([b"private compressed bytes"])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT,
        transport=httpx.MockTransport(
            lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, headers={"Content-Encoding": "gzip"}, stream=chunks)
        ),
    )
    assert run(client)["reason"] == "access_decision_invalid"
    assert chunks.read == 0 and chunks.closed
```



## 通用架构、状态机与开发调试台

业务事实以模块返回的 projection.state 为准，个人流程 cursor 和任务 Run.status 分开处理。workflow 可以声明 state_machine；完整 Schema 随契约附录导出。它描述业务状态、转移及 step_id/action_id 引用，校验器检查重复、起点、端点与动作关系。guard_description 仅供说明，权限和状态守卫仍由模块后端执行；不得以浏览器改状态或模型文字代替业务事务。无状态机的历史流程显示未声明，不用导航步骤冒充状态。

模块开发完成后使用 /#devtools 检查 capability / interaction / workflow 的定义、引用版本、前端安装情况与可用性。JSON 校验不安装、不执行源码、不访问网络引用；在线交互 state_schema 正则不执行，含该类规则时到离线受控校验。调试台仅平台管理员可用，且只观察本人流程和当前有权业务对象；每2秒轮询、暂停/手动刷新、图与正式 FlowHost 并排。观察历史最多100条，仅当前对象的状态元数据，不含正文与草稿，也不是完整业务事件审计。源码已安装与浏览器构建已加载分别核实。

优先用 framework-lab 中立模块演练：创建→提交→退回→修改→重提→完成，页面/对话共用步骤组件和对象。它是本人调试样例，不是允许自审的真实审批规则。Node workflow-node 模板也从共享flow状态声明执行转移；其他模块可用自有业务状态引擎，仍返回统一投影。

架构与迁移入口见 docs/通用框架架构与代码地图.md、docs/模块开发与调试说明书.md、docs/框架迁移与AI交接指南.md。scripts/export_framework.py 从正向源码清单导出全新通用包，仅安装 framework-lab，重建提示词与说明书，不复制业务数据/凭据/远程连接/行业演示账号/依赖/构建物/Git历史。新包仍需安装锁定依赖、构建并实际验收；导出摘要不是运行验收。
